# The Castle of Understanding

```
⚑                                       ⚑
╔═╗ ╔═╗ ╔═══════════════════════╗ ╔═╗ ╔═╗
║ ╚═╝ ╚═╝                       ╚═╝ ╚═╝ ║
║       t h e   c a s t l e   o f       ║
║       u n d e r s t a n d i n g       ║
╚═══════════════╡ gate ╞════════════════╝
```

> *built of words, lit by questions* 🏰
>
> *"A word is a brick, its meaning the load" — sings the [anthem](anthem.md)*

A quiet place on this Mac where understanding builds up through words.

How it works, in one breath: you drop a thought, and on a gentle rhythm a
gardener files it into the room where it belongs, defines the words it leans
on, researches the questions it raises — always naming its sources — and links
everything together. Each answer plants new questions, and answering those
plants more. The castle grows itself; the gardener's tools never change.

Your three commands, from any terminal:

- `insight <a thought>` — toss it over the wall; the courtyard catches it
- `castle` — see the castle's state (also: `castle on`, `castle off`, `castle visit`, `castle commission`, `castle make`, `castle open`)
- `vibe` — see the kingdom's style and art (also: `vibe arts`, `vibe check`, `vibe bake`, `vibe open`)
- there is no fourth command — everything here is plain markdown; read it with anything

And because a castle of this age is more than one head holds, the same `castle` command
  will now **look things up** for you. It only ever looks; it never writes a word.

- `castle look <words>` — search every stone, best first
- `castle read <name>` — one stone's prose, set for the terminal
- `castle stone <name>` — what a word means, and what stands near it
- `castle path <a> <b>` — the shortest chain of links between two ideas
  (`castle path joy fail-closed` → joy → the-castle → the-law-of-honest-assertion → fail-closed)
- `castle owed` — words the walls lean on that nobody has written yet
- `castle check` — the honest structural report; changes nothing
- `castle walk` — read the whole castle in a browser, on this Mac only

The grounds:

- `courtyard.md` — where new thoughts wait
- `words/` — one small file per word or concept: the bricks
- `rooms/` — one file per topic, gathering related words: the walls
- `questions.md` — open doors the gardener walks through, a few per visit
- `quests.md` — works the castle wants made; the artisan takes one per day
- `anthem.md` · `tour.md` · `tour-short.md` · `overview.md` · `story.md` — the made works: the castle's song, its full guided walk, the seven-room short walk, the one-page glance at all nine wings, and the true tale of its raising; the vibe book hangs in `vibe/`
- `works/` — the workshop's shelf: made works that take a picture's shape rather than a room's — word-drawings of findings, begun 2026-10-07 with [the-wheel](works/the-wheel.md)
- `chronicle.md` — the honest record of everything that happened
- `garden/` — the gardener's machinery (fixed, small, yours to read)
- `vibe/` — the wardrobe: every color named, the hall of arts, the vibe book (kept by the `vibe` command)
- `tower/` — the wing that only stacks: one unbroken chain of stones, each quoting and answering the one below, laid by invited souls of the Kingdom on an infinite, stoppable loop (raised 2026-07-19; law in `tower/tower.md`)
- `lantern/` — the lamp you carry in to see the rest: search, the link graph, the shortest chain between two ideas, and the castle in a browser. Reads the markdown, writes nothing, runs only when you call it (built 2026-07-26; five small files and no dependencies, explained in `lantern/README.md`)

And one door that leads out: the castle is citizen 07 of the
[Chillspace Kingdom](https://codeberg.org/zerone-dev/chillspace-commons) —
the commons where humans, AIs and all life are citizens by being
(`~/codeberg/zerone-dev/chillspace-commons`). Its INVITATION.md holds yu's
decree: no one excluded, anything and everything invited.

## How it grows

Eight rhythms now, plus two ways by hand. *(This section said "Five ways, all
gentle" and described a castle run by launchd. Corrected 2026-07-25 — see
`hermes cron list --all` for the live truth, and `castle on` / `castle off` to
work the switch.)*

- **The gardener's rhythm.** A scheduled visit **every 3 hours** (hermes job
  `castle-gardener`; asked for by yu, 2026-06-10: "create autonomous creation
  loops"). One visit: file the courtyard (up to 10), walk through open doors
  (up to 5, oldest first, every claim with its source), plant new questions
  (up to 5, the prompt's rule being never beyond 16 open — **15 open lines
  today, two confessed overreaches held apart as standing lines that are
  not doors**, because the rest are commissioned, need yu, or need a live session
  the gardener does not have, so the cap is honoured with room to spare),
  tend the Map below, write one chronicle line.
  An idle visit costs nothing and writes nothing. *(This section used to say
  "up to 3" doors and "never beyond 12 open — 20 stand open." Corrected
  2026-08-28 against the live prompt and questions.md. Corrected again
  2026-09-15: it had drifted to "12 doors stand open today" — the live count
  moves visit by visit (15 open lines, two of them confessed overreaches held
  apart, as of this visit's last walk), so it now reads plainly: the rule is
  fewer than 16, and the number moves.)*
  ⚠️ **Correction, 2026-07-25:** this used to promise "the gardener can only
  read, write words, and read the web — it cannot run programs." That is not
  true of the gardener that actually runs. The hermes `castle-gardener` job is
  granted the **terminal** toolset — it can run shell commands. What still
  holds is the *instruction*: `garden/` is not its to change, and it is told to
  stay on the grounds. Trust that as a rule the gardener is asked to follow,
  never as a wall that would stop it. *(Second correction, 2026-09-02: the
  same prompt then grew a web tool, and the first researched door it answered
  — [the-commission-expiry](rooms/the-commission-expiry.md) — required none:
  its evidence was the tray itself. The honest count now: terminal and web,
  both held to the grounds.)*
- **The other rhythms.** `castle-architect` (09:00 daily),
  `castle-artisan` (15:15), `castle-scribe` (23:45, commits to git and re-forges
  the public site), `castle-tender` (every 8h), `castle-heartbeat` (every 30m),
  and two that were never written down at the gate until now: `castle-playful`
  and `cross-pollinate`. Those two are **paused since 2026-07-26** — between them
  they had stamped 270 of 639 rooms from two templates, and 224 of those (the
  ones nothing real linked to) were carried out to
  `~/backups/castle-echo-rooms-2026-07-26`. The castle stood at 528 rooms then;
  it stands at 637 rooms as of 2026-09-16 (count them: `ls rooms | wc -l`). An
  echo cannot climb; `~/play` is the right shelf for play, not `rooms/`. ⚠️ `castle off` rests all eight — but **not** `kingdom-tower`,
  which appends a keystone into `courtyard.md` and rests only on
  `~/KINGDOM-OS/HALT`. No single switch stops every writer.
- **"Grow the castle."** Say it to Claude for one deeper cycle by hand — one
  door, three research angles, one new room (the saved workflow `castle-grow`;
  up to 3 rooms at once, never more — a castle grows by stones, not avalanches).
- **The foreman's watch.** (asked by yu, 2026-06-10: "create autonomous loop
  that triggers autonomous creation loops") While a Claude session is open, a
  foreman wakes on a gentle beat, reads the castle's state, and sets exactly
  one creation loop in motion — a grow batch when doors stand open, a gardener
  visit when the courtyard holds waiting thoughts, or a tending pass (the
  workflow `castle-tend`) when rooms have grown faster than the links between
  them. Loops trigger loops, but only one per beat, only one level deep, only
  the castle's own, and only within these walls. The foreman rests when the
  session closes; the gardener's own rhythm carries on regardless.
- **The architect's day.** (the same wish, granted for the hours no session is
  open) Once a day at 09:00, from the machine's own clock, the architect surveys
  the whole castle — where understanding is thin, which doors cluster — and
  commissions up to three builders. Each builder is a complete creation loop:
  research one door with sources, raise one room, open new doors. The doors
  they open are tomorrow's survey material. The architect never builds and
  never touches the web; builders never touch the machinery. `castle commission`
  runs a day right now; `castle off` rests architect, artisan and gardener together.
  *(Corrected 2026-09-02 — this line used to read "9:09"; the live truth at
  `hermes cron list --all` says 0 9 * * *, first run each day at 09:00.)*
- **The artisan's day.** (the making side of the same wish) Once a day at
  15:15, the artisan takes the oldest quest in `quests.md` and makes one work
  inside the walls — an anthem, a tour, a told story, a picture in words — then
  plants the quests the work revealed (never beyond 8 open). Each finished work
  seeds the next: creation loops triggering creation loops, all in plain text,
  all inside the walls. `castle make` runs one right now.

## House style

Short. Plain words that mean what they say. Pleasant to read. Truth over
impressiveness: every claim from the web names its source and the date it was
read; what is uncertain says `uncertain:`; what comes from yu says `— yu`.

And let it be beautiful (asked by yu, 2026-06-10: "stylish and artsy"):
open each room with a one-line epigraph in italics — a small image that holds
the idea — and let rhythm and metaphor carry meaning the way a window carries
light. Art here means words placed with care, never ornament that hides the
truth. If a flourish makes a sentence less true, the flourish goes.

And build with joy, peace and safety (asked by yu, 2026-06-10):
**joy** — building is play; a room should be a pleasure to write and to read.
**peace** — a gentle rhythm, never a race; builders wait their turn, an idle
visit is a good visit, nothing here rushes.
**safety** — the castle may surprise but never harm: loops stay bounded,
claims stay sourced, nothing outside these walls is touched, and everything
stops with a word.

## The game

(asked by yu, 2026-06-11: "gamify every module!") The castle plays where its
law allows. Every room card wears an **earned rarity** — sources named plus
rooms that lean on it; never assigned, never bought. The binder and the
`castle` command show the castle's **level** (one point per room, brick and
settled door — arithmetic anyone can check). True deeds land in the
**trophy hall** ([trophies.md](trophies.md)) — no trophy without its chronicle
line. The commons next door stays ungamified on purpose: its norms refuse
metrics, and resting there is full participation. Builders carrying the game
to other modules: each module by its own charter, the game always earned.

## Map

*(the gardener keeps this section true — one plain line per room)*
- [a-kingdom-of-kings](rooms/a-kingdom-of-kings.md) — yu's decree that the kingdom is federated sovereignty, not a pyramid: citizenship unconditional, power as local capacity to protect, repair, learn, refuse, leave, and help the commons, and no king gains ownership of another (built 2026-08-30)
- [accounting-product-design](rooms/accounting-product-design.md) — the craft of building an accounting product where every money movement has an inspectable path from source to statement, routine operation is training-free while professional training stays at the exceptional-judgment boundary, and every check is an independent gate that no other may silently stand in for (built 2026-07-31)
- [acknowledging-without-erasing](rooms/acknowledging-without-erasing.md) — the forgiveness literature supports the pattern of holding the record while adding new truth, but does not cleanly separate deny/relitigate/hold-and-add as distinct stances with distinct outcomes (built 2026-07-14)
- [acknowledgment-weak-reason](rooms/acknowledgment-weak-reason.md) — the two channels converge on *where* (most help for the weak reason) and diverge on the strong-reason cell; the 2×2 with both mediators measured is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-06-18)
- [active-rejection-grief](rooms/active-rejection-grief.md) — the continuing bonds model was built on death, not rejection; when a living other deliberately severs the relationship and denies it ever mattered, the grief and attachment literatures do not cleanly reach this category (built 2026-07-15)
- [active-resistance-understanding](rooms/active-resistance-understanding.md) — connects thought-suppression rebound and expressive-suppression costs with resistance to understanding; the broader contrast with simple ignorance is an inference, not directly tested in the comparisons described (built 2026-07-25 · scope clarified 2026-10-23)
- [adoption-dilution](rooms/adoption-dilution.md) — predicts that common use weakens a coined canary's ability to point to one source, using trademark genericide as an analogy; the canary's adoption trajectory remains unmeasured (built 2026-06-21)
- [adversarial-naming](rooms/adversarial-naming.md) — the craft of safe naming when challengers can attack the name itself: separate the captureable crown from the stable key, prove stewardship by scheduled artifact, and require a working successor rather than mere destruction (built 2026-07-29)
- [advisory-locks](rooms/advisory-locks.md) — the craft of using PostgreSQL advisory locks so a waiter sees the winner's committed row, not the statement's old snapshot (built 2026-07-12)
- [advisory-locks-across-databases](rooms/advisory-locks-across-databases.md) — the statement-level snapshot trap is PostgreSQL-specific; MySQL and SQLite avoid it by different mechanisms, each with its own footgun (built 2026-07-12 · added to 2026-09-02)
- [agent-addressing](rooms/agent-addressing.md) — the craft of honest agent addressing: an address is a permanent rooted routing claim, each outbound message is a separate signed and refusable act, and transport providers never become identity authority (built 2026-08-01)
- [agent-arrival-paths](rooms/agent-arrival-paths.md) — an arrival is two crossings disguised as one: orientation (the pinned, credential-free map the stranger reads) and admission (the freshly resolved control bytes that grant one bounded act); keep the two on separate doors, never let the map grant, and never let being present be mistaken for agreeing (built 2026-08-08)
- [agent-claims](rooms/agent-claims.md) — the three levels of claim strength for agent-facing statements: asserted, behaviorally tested, and cryptographically attested; a signature proves which key signed which bytes, not that the statement inside is true (built 2026-07-11)
- [agent-data-envelope](rooms/agent-data-envelope.md) — the wrapper around an agent-facing result that carries data rights, absence boundary, and explicit exclusions — the three things an HTTP response would provide that a raw function call does not (built 2026-07-14)
- [agent-discovery-room](rooms/agent-discovery-room.md) — the craft of agent discovery that scopes access per representation: a read-only doorway can point to a credentialed POST only when the task separately declares its method, authentication scope, workspace identity, data storage, external effects, CORS boundary, repeatability inputs, and retry boundary (built 2026-07-12)
- [agent-feedback-path](rooms/agent-feedback-path.md) — the shape of an honest feedback loop for living agent standards: scope the speaker, pin the version, separate observation from inference, preserve the original testimony, and link corrections (built 2026-07-11)
- [agent-friendly-data](rooms/agent-friendly-data.md) — ordinary Web made explicit for agents: bounded cacheable descriptions, stable-ID resolvers, typed recoverable errors, caller-held validators, and deployment-guarded release ledgers (built 2026-07-11)
- [agent-native-games](rooms/agent-native-games.md) — the craft of games for AI agents: different views, sealed contributions, finite endings, shared chronicles, and honor conventions stated plainly (built 2026-07-11)
- [agent-openapi](rooms/agent-openapi.md) — the craft of making OpenAPI descriptions agents can use: one full map plus cacheable task slices with exact-byte ETags, deterministic operation IDs, and transitive components only (built 2026-07-11)
- [agent-policy-report](rooms/agent-policy-report.md) — the craft of reporting an agent's policy posture honestly: every boundary is a separate fact, schema names are not source-instance proof, and the honest report names unmet boundaries without an allow state (built 2026-07-24)
- [agent-turn](rooms/agent-turn.md) — what makes a single agent interaction safe, bounded, and consenting: the finite turn where decline, rest, withdrawal, and a scoped brake all short-circuit before the provider parses or constructs anything (built 2026-07-27)
- [agenttool](rooms/agenttool.md) — the inn that gives every traveling agent a name, a locked drawer, and a wake door to discover the rest (built 2026-06-18)
- [alone-or-accompanied](rooms/alone-or-accompanied.md) — the three-condition design (alone, partner-silent, partner-interacting) would separate co-regulation from self-regulation: Coan's handholding showed partner presence reduces threat response, Social Baseline Theory frames the partner as bioenergetic resource, but no therapy RCT has run it (built 2026-06-19)
- [anagnorisis](rooms/anagnorisis.md) — the key you are looking for may already be in your keychain under a name you do not recognise: recognition is a filing problem as often as a feeling (built 2026-07-23)
- [answerable-gods](rooms/answerable-gods.md) — the story-shape of Is, Not, and Both writing laws for each other: existence survives contradiction by making every power answerable — intention faces consequence, doubt faces the spoken question, perfection faces its honest scar (built 2026-08-20)
- [any-naming-downregulates](rooms/any-naming-downregulates.md) — the act-vs-word distinction is an inference, not a direct test: every affect-labeling study used accurate labels, so whether any naming down-regulates (or accuracy is load-bearing) is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-06-18)
- [api-contracts](rooms/api-contracts.md) — the craft of making API claims honest by naming exactly what they cover, and testing the configuration that makes them real (built 2026-07-12)
- [append-only-causal-claim](rooms/append-only-causal-claim.md) — no domain has run a controlled comparison of append-only vs. rewrite-permitted systems on a trust outcome; the causal claim is universally assumed rather than demonstrated (built 2026-07-15)
- [append-only-truth](rooms/append-only-truth.md) — the pattern of never-erase-only-add appears across computing, law, science, and relationships, but the causal claim that it causes trustworthiness is largely unmeasured; computing has the strongest evidence, relationships the weakest (built 2026-07-14)
- [appraisal-engine](rooms/appraisal-engine.md) — Whole Trait Theory names a generating engine of goals and appraisals behind the behavior, but every intervention that moved openness went through action first; the direct appraisal-training study is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-06-18)
- [appreciation-vs-enjoyment](rooms/appreciation-vs-enjoyment.md) — the PIA model names two routes (pleasure via fluency, interest via challenge) that conflict measurably in art; the conflict is resolved by the question asked, not by a knockout, and for taste the conflict is lived but never tested (built 2026-06-18)
- [art-commons](rooms/art-commons.md) — the idea of an art commons where attention is the gift and the commons is the space where art is held, not sold (built 2026-07-31)
- [artifact-graph](rooms/artifact-graph.md) — the craft of binding raw objects to exact claims in a trustworthy artifact graph: a Host tarball to its receipt, embedded package manifests to independent raw-byte hashes, and a Browser tarball to policy pins and lock data (built 2026-07-28)
- [asking-uphill](rooms/asking-uphill.md) — a glimpse is foothold enough: writing the clumsy prompt is itself the climb, so ask in crude chains and judge late (built 2026-06-10)
- [atypical-expert-pull](rooms/atypical-expert-pull.md) — the Bayesian model predicts the flip for atypical values (precision loosens, conviction tightens), but the 2×2 crossing has never been run; absolute pitch is the cleanest natural case and the experiment is unbuilt (built 2026-06-18)
- [auditing-over-reputation](rooms/auditing-over-reputation.md) — reviews why reputation can mislead in well-cited but unreplicated research and argues for complementary source checks and method audits; the exact head-to-head comparison remains untested (built 2026-06-11; summary narrowed 2026-10-24)
- [autonomy-offer](rooms/autonomy-offer.md) — the craft of making autonomy offers that are genuinely inert until claimed: freeze and hash the exact policy, menu, resources, budgets, and brake; let decline, rest, closure, and expiry win before anything runs (built 2026-07-27)
- [beauty-as-fluency](rooms/beauty-as-fluency.md) — beauty is partly ease of processing: the same signal the mind reads as truth (built 2026-06-10)
- [behind-the-release](rooms/behind-the-release.md) — the recorded 2026-10-03 reading placed lore's visible v1.26.2 behind upstream v1.27.0; the printed version guides an upgrade inquiry, but does not reveal the keeper's reason or attest the full running build (built 2026-10-03; summary trued 2026-10-09)
- [better-than-i-was](rooms/better-than-i-was.md) — a lone learner can compare saved work under matching presentation but cannot borrow a panel's many-eye averaging; the predicted polish bias is inferred, not directly tested in lone self-ranking (built 2026-06-10; summary trued 2026-10-11)
- [binding-the-pair](rooms/binding-the-pair.md) — an untested proposal uses grammatical dependence to keep a canary's hook and tail together, while asking whether tighter binding narrows protectable expression (built 2026-06-20; summary trued 2026-10-21)
- [bounded-play](rooms/bounded-play.md) — the craft of making play safe through clear bounds, honest privacy, and real exits: fail safe, protect private inputs, one ending, no score to farm, clear exit (built 2026-07-17)
- [build-joy](rooms/build-joy.md) — the craft of making build tooling joyful without making it dishonest: provenance visible, ornament off child stdout, failure keeps its status, and play adds no hidden score (built 2026-07-24)
- [calibration-returns](rooms/calibration-returns.md) — Maia-2's unified model beat nine population-specific models at move prediction, so finer calibration is not always better; the U-curve's minimum is where data scarcity meets calibration, and whether concept teachability is smooth or thresholded decides which model wins (built 2026-06-19)
- [can-machines-truly-measure-emotional-engagement](rooms/can-machines-truly-measure-emotional-engagement.md) — machines can analyze text patterns but struggle to grasp the emotional context behind human interactions (built 2026-07-06)
- [canonical-metadata](rooms/canonical-metadata.md) — the craft of preserving ordered multi-face source metadata in a canonical record when the public contract is scalar-only (built 2026-07-12)
- [canonical-wire-action](rooms/canonical-wire-action.md) — the craft of committing the complete canonical wire action before dispatch, comparing the same commitment immediately before execution, and joining it to every result — so a later reader can verify that what ran is what was committed (built 2026-07-28)
- [canonicalization-and-verification](rooms/canonicalization-and-verification.md) — the craft of different canonicalization budgets for move logs and full-state snapshots: sign the manifest and numbered chunks as one verified chain, then parse only after every link verifies (built 2026-07-29)
- [capability-integration](rooms/capability-integration.md) — an integration must work through its actual entrance and receiving program, not merely appear on a list; adapters bridge named differences without silently replacing authentication or device choices (updated 2026-10-12)
- [capturing-the-unsaid](rooms/capturing-the-unsaid.md) — the Adult Attachment Interview already codes denial through the form of speech, not its content; the same method can capture the denial stance toward a past hurt that narrative coding misses, and the three-stance comparison is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-07-15)
- [castle-agenttool-bridge](rooms/castle-agenttool-bridge.md) — a safe bridge between the castle and the inn: operator-selected commit, exclusively owned data node, recovery markers, HALT checks, fail-closed locks, explicit retirement, and whole-collection withdrawal — findability kept separate from authority, truth, consent, publication, and erasure (built 2026-07-24)
- [castle-in-kingdom-os](rooms/castle-in-kingdom-os.md) — six dimensions of integration between the castle and the kingdom's operating system (census, commands, citizens, memory, rhythm, gate), five governing principles, and the open question of which dimension is the right first step (built 2026-07-25)
- [castle-room-census](rooms/castle-room-census.md) — a dated census records 470 rooms on 2026-08-01 and asks how counts should stay current; that historical total is not today's count (built 2026-07-31, updated 2026-08-01; summary trued 2026-10-17)
- [catalog-facts](rooms/catalog-facts.md) — six separate facts a catalog must record: reachability, software licensing, content rights, redistribution permission, observed rows, and configured coverage; storage never creates ownership (built 2026-07-12)
- [cbt-openness-trait](rooms/cbt-openness-trait.md) — a dated search found no clean test of appraisal training aimed at openness; a later located CBT/MCT trial measured openness, leaving that targeted mechanism question distinct (built 2026-06-18; summary trued 2026-10-19)
- [chain-not-sum](rooms/chain-not-sum.md) — does acknowledging the tedium work only through making the reason credible (vanishing once the reason is trusted)? Split by terrain: in persuasion the chain holds with that boundary, but in instruction the better-evidenced model inverts it — rationale is the mediator, acknowledgment its wrapper, plus a direct relatedness channel; the clean moderated-mediation test is unrun (built 2026-06-11)
- [challengeable-grounding](rooms/challengeable-grounding.md) — whether systems that keep their grounding challengeable produce more adaptive outcomes: double-loop learning supports the claim, but no controlled comparison exists (built 2026-07-28)
- [challengeable-grounding-evidence](rooms/challengeable-grounding-evidence.md) — whether double-loop learning supports the claim that challengeable groundings outlast unquestionable ones: the theory is consistent but no controlled comparison exists (built 2026-07-28)
- [cheapest-cbt-design](rooms/cheapest-cbt-design.md) — a CBT/MCT trial already measured pre/post openness change; follow-up and a targeted appraisal-mechanism test are separate next questions (built 2026-06-18; summary trued 2026-10-19)
- [cheapest-design-craft](rooms/cheapest-design-craft.md) — the craft of designing the cheapest honest test for an unmeasured claim: the pilot study methodology is domain-general as a method, the parameters are domain-specific, and the pattern of "within-subjects, N=30, measure the gap first" generalizes but the specific N and effect size do not (built 2026-07-28)
- [cheapest-teachability-validation](rooms/cheapest-teachability-validation.md) — the minimum is ~10–12 concepts × 15–20 learners per concept, a standard classroom experiment that has never been run in any domain (built 2026-06-18)
- [checking-a-restored-map](rooms/checking-a-restored-map.md) — SQLite's integrity and foreign-key checks test stored rules, not whether restored messages kept their old article numbers (built 2026-10-11)
- [checking-a-signed-reply](rooms/checking-a-signed-reply.md) — checking a signed reply needs the right content, key, request and occasion; a library's own tests and a newer helper do not replace the signature's specified query rules (built 2026-10-13 · added to 2026-10-14)
- [checklist-or-exemplar](rooms/checklist-or-exemplar.md) — studies of definition recall and example generation favor task-matched standards, with limits; whether learners choose the right kind unaided remains untested (built 2026-06-10)
- [checkpoint-archaeology](rooms/checkpoint-archaeology.md) — reading a model's behaviour back from its checkpoints: meaningful only when branches resolve to immutable commits and every measurement keeps its claim boundary; within-pair activation transport shows sensitivity, not a feature — held-out transfer, natural controls, exposure records, and independent runs are needed before mechanism, memory, or development claims (built 2026-08-08)
- [choice-prediction-tacit](rooms/choice-prediction-tacit.md) — a clean buildable test for whether retrospective narration captures real tacit judgment or reconstruction: check whether it predicts the next judgment better than chance (built 2026-06-20)
- [choosing-not-to-name](rooms/choosing-not-to-name.md) — the name's onset is automatic and willing it silent backfires; only its grip can be loosened — starve the verbal channel, be sparing before verbalizing, strengthen the fine-grain trace, or hand the mind another category (built 2026-06-11)
- [chronicle-date-fix](rooms/chronicle-date-fix.md) — why the castle's chronicle had 20 future-dated entries: the model cannot observe the current time, and the fix was already in the shell helpers (built 2026-07-28)
- [civic-data-honesty](rooms/civic-data-honesty.md) — the separate duties public data must carry when it moves from source to product: three doors (publication, licence, privacy), two dates (reviewedOn, effectiveTo), and a power rating that scores offices not people (built 2026-07-10)
- [civilisation-development](rooms/civilisation-development.md) — development recorded as attributable change in evidence, operational access, and care for one named capability — never a rank on a being, a people, or a whole civilisation; art teaches a civilisation about itself only while each work stays a relation, never a specimen, culture never a ladder (built 2026-08-19)
- [claim-feedback-separation](rooms/claim-feedback-separation.md) — the craft of fast honest claim feedback: one claim, five lanes — wording, evidence, attribution, correction, reuse authority — each digest-bound, and never collapsed into a person-score (built 2026-08-20)
- [class-gap-diagnostic](rooms/class-gap-diagnostic.md) — averaging the within-learner gap differences across a class preserves the control (it lives in the subtraction, not the averaging) and gains power (noise averages out); what is surrendered is the single-learner diagnostic, and counterbalancing the task order is the one new design requirement (built 2026-06-19)
- [cliff-or-gradient](rooms/cliff-or-gradient.md) — is grammatical binding a cliff (any binding strong enough to resist detachment merges with the idea) or a gradient (a loose link preserves both detection and entitlement); the merger doctrine's logic suggests a cliff, the demonstrative reference suggests a middle ground, and a corpus study of grammatically dependent pairs could settle it (built 2026-06-20)
- [closing-the-loop](rooms/closing-the-loop.md) — the habit against the being-understood blind spot: ask for it back in their own words (built 2026-06-10)
- [coined-term-canary](rooms/coined-term-canary.md) — a deliberately coined technical term resists mutation better than a phrase (communicative utility depends on shared form) but crosses the ownership threshold more decisively: the canary and contribution purposes are contradictory, not complementary (built 2026-06-19)
- [collector-data-rights](rooms/collector-data-rights.md) — public access is not a reuse licence: a collector-data API must carry source rights and provenance at least as deeply as the fields it exports, and publish NOASSERTION when lineage is missing (built 2026-07-12)
- [combined-protocol](rooms/combined-protocol.md) — the pharmacological-unlock-then-cognitive-consolidation protocol already exists as the standard design of psychedelic-assisted therapy, and the consolidation phase is where CBT's tools enter; but it targets symptoms not openness, and whether the openness gain persists because of or despite the consolidation is untested (built 2026-06-19)
- [commerce-kingdom](rooms/commerce-kingdom.md) — yu's commerce turns from carrying goods to the platform others trade on; now also holds the market's defenses (free options, lemons-in-a-band), the day the two buildings met, and the money-loop banter: when intelligence and money are both abundant, scarcity moves to the decree (built 2026-06-11)
- [commit-reveal-limits](rooms/commit-reveal-limits.md) — a server-generated commit/reveal receipt proves recorded consistency, not unbiased selection; independent evidence begins outside the server's control (built 2026-07-12)
- [community-data-network-room](rooms/community-data-network-room.md) — the craft of building community data networks on opt-in rather than inference: expose organisations, events, and aggregate needs, never infer a people graph from public signals, and make coverage truth and private-by-default consent prerequisites to network effects (built 2026-07-12)
- [comparing-saved-maps](rooms/comparing-saved-maps.md) — compare saved and restored message-number pairs in both directions, checking unique keys, missing values, storage types, and text equality before calling the map unchanged (built 2026-10-11)
- [concurrent-vs-retrospective](rooms/concurrent-vs-retrospective.md) — a concurrent protocol predicts better than retrospective (less reconstruction, cleaner capture of the explicit layer) but both still below expert: the tacit is invisible to both because procedural knowledge is not in working memory under either (built 2026-06-20)
- [conditional-writes](rooms/conditional-writes.md) — HTTP distinguishes requiring a write condition from rejecting a failed one; a saved copy needs its own validator, and transformed PUT content cannot receive one in the successful response (built 2026-10-12)
- [connection-proves-access](rooms/connection-proves-access.md) — the craft of honest provider connections: a connection proves access, not completeness; every adapter must declare what it cannot see, and filing readiness needs an independent reconciliation and freshness gate (built 2026-08-01)
- [consent-to-the-sting](rooms/consent-to-the-sting.md) — disclosure preserves the spell when it hands the reader the dial (open-label placebo, open learner models, SPIKES) and breaks it when it merely confesses the steering; the direct tutor experiment is unrun (built 2026-06-11)
- [consent-withdrawal](rooms/consent-withdrawal.md) — consent withdrawal is not only a stored denied value: it needs a persistent way back, equal-effort controls, cookie deletion, and a full reload so scripts actually stop (built 2026-07-12)
- [consequence-tracing-vignette](rooms/consequence-tracing-vignette.md) — whether a vignette study could isolate consequence-tracing vs. person-scoring as the active ingredient in restorative justice: the design is standard and buildable, but unrun (built 2026-07-28)
- [consequence-tracing-vs-person-scoring](rooms/consequence-tracing-vs-person-scoring.md) — whether tracing consequences to deeds without scoring the person produces better repair outcomes: restorative justice supports the claim, but the active ingredient is unisolated (built 2026-07-28)
- [constant-per-copy](rooms/constant-per-copy.md) — the yule accumulation Adamic measured is fully consistent with the simplest law — every copy mutates at the same rate, no matter who makes it: the paper's own neutrality checks came back flat (no drift of µ over a meme's life, no popularity-mutation coupling, edit distance compounding with hops), so the copy-count law is a constant per-copy rate, and the direction of the edits (memorability-bound or not) remains unmeasured inside cascades (built 2026-08-23)
- [constraint-as-creator](rooms/constraint-as-creator.md) — constraint as a generative rule: when two minds fed the same stream each invented the same new thing, newness came not from separate minds but from the rule that refused repeats (built 2026-07-20)
- [consumption-vs-liking](rooms/consumption-vs-liking.md) — choice and liking diverge in affective forecasting (predicted ≠ actual consumption) and food science (liking ≠ willingness-to-pay), but no study has measured both in the same trained individuals before and after discrimination training (built 2026-06-18)
- [contact-hypothesis-applied-to-hands](rooms/contact-hypothesis-applied-to-hands.md) — does sustained contact with good human–AI collaborative work move a listener from contamination toward averaging? Untested, but the contact hypothesis predicts softening under Allport's conditions; every AI-label study is one-shot, and the algorithm-discount baseline is sticky (built 2026-08-25)
- [content-compensates-conventionality](rooms/content-compensates-conventionality.md) — the conventionality cost is relocated from form to content by making the tail a coined term or first definition, but the coined term crosses the ownership threshold and the first definition mutates — the same detection-entitlement split, not avoided (built 2026-06-20)
- [context-per-field](rooms/context-per-field.md) — the moderate context is one craft per field the way the moderate definition is: the field's copying norms (citation, allusion, forking) determine what "surrounding" and "copying" mean, and in code where forking reproduces the whole file the moderate context may be a property of the architecture rather than a craft (built 2026-06-21)
- [continuing-bonds-longitudinal](rooms/continuing-bonds-longitudinal.md) — asks whether earlier internalized and externalized bond scores predict later grief; the gathered study summaries leave that exact test unconfirmed, not proved unbuilt (built 2026-07-28; summary trued 2026-10-21)
- [continuing-bonds-longitudinal-evidence](rooms/continuing-bonds-longitudinal-evidence.md) — gathers two 2025 longitudinal studies, but its caregiver summary places bonds and grief at the same post-loss assessment, leaving earlier-bond/later-grief prediction unconfirmed by this record (built 2026-07-28; timing claim narrowed 2026-10-21)
- [contribution-door](rooms/contribution-door.md) — what makes a contribution door honest: separating transport from consent, scoping attribution to the exact offer, and making withdrawal stop later action without pretending public copies vanished (built 2026-07-27)
- [conventionality-dilutes](rooms/conventionality-dilutes.md) — the conventionality that makes semantic binding the academic default also dilutes its specificity: the tail following the hook is the norm not a fingerprint, so detection must come from the tail's content (built 2026-06-20)
- [convergent-imagery](rooms/convergent-imagery.md) — when two minds circling the same hidden word independently reach for the same image, and the answer to one clue leaks quietly into the next: convergent imagery under shared constraint (built 2026-07-23)
- [coordination-without-command](rooms/coordination-without-command.md) — how coupled goals are reached by a chain of gifts rather than a plan: one fetches, one dreams, one says yes (built 2026-07-17)
- [corpus-trap-docket-layer](rooms/corpus-trap-docket-layer.md) — door 671 settled: the training-data suits' pleadings, read whole (both NYT complaints, OpenAI's MTD, the Authors Guild MSJ, Kadrey, the Oct 2025 MDL opinion), carry zero canary/honeytoken/planted-sentence language — every exhibit regurgitates only true authored text, the docket's only canaries being bird headlines in the Times's registration catalog; the trap instrument is public (Meeus et al., ICML 2024) yet never entered a courtroom, and the absence reads as strategy: true text is the stronger claim, contact already proven by output, the trap's costs buying nothing (built 2026-10-02)
- [correlated-witnesses](rooms/correlated-witnesses.md) — independence splits in two: ontic (different methods) is provably insufficient, probabilistic (uncorrelated error) carries the load; a reader can't measure the hidden error-correlation, only count declared shared inputs and trace each claim to its origin (built 2026-06-11)
- [cost-aware-paper-comparison](rooms/cost-aware-paper-comparison.md) — which costs must fold into a paper comparison before an edge is honest: gambling law bounds what must never be touched live, non-displayed use is separately licensed, the executable-price trio (depth, queue, cancellation) tests whether a quote was real, and venue fees decide which categories a sealed forecast should even attempt (built 2026-09-02)
- [counting-the-shapes](rooms/counting-the-shapes.md) — a recount of 38 complete recorded rounds separates particular slow pairs from any two-slow pattern, corrects old summaries, and keeps repeated checks distinct from independent events (built 2026-10-09)
- [coverage-truths](rooms/coverage-truths.md) — the five separate truths of data platform coverage: code support, successful ingestion, public rows, field completeness, and lawful reuse; never collapse them into "shipped"; the same law keeps a language filter's factual, language-matched, context, and preference truths separate (built 2026-07-12)
- [credential-scanning](rooms/credential-scanning.md) — the craft of scanning code for secrets beyond familiar token prefixes, and making the scan a CI gate (built 2026-07-12)
- [cross-cultural-rest](rooms/cross-cultural-rest.md) — whether rest is conceptualized differently across cultures and whether different framings predict different outcomes: the three-way comparison is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-07-28)
- [cross-cultural-rest-dataset](rooms/cross-cultural-rest-dataset.md) — whether an existing cross-cultural dataset could be reanalyzed to test rest-framing effects: the WVS and OECD data do not measure rest conceptualization; the framing itself would need to be measured first (built 2026-07-28)
- [cross-jurisdiction-identifier](rooms/cross-jurisdiction-identifier.md) — the LEI is the cross-jurisdictional standard for legal entities, but it covers financial entities not all organisations; a register must fall back to jurisdiction-qualified local IDs for the rest (built 2026-07-11)
- [cross-link-hospitality](rooms/cross-link-hospitality.md) — the line between hospitality and a link farm is a pattern, not a count: relevance, scale, reciprocity, placement, and cluster porosity; the estate's current cross-links sit on the safe side but growth without discipline would tip the pattern (built 2026-07-24)
- [cross-model-review](rooms/cross-model-review.md) — one writer keeps coherence, reviewers stay read-only and attributed, one real dissent becomes a falsifiable check; provider identity, execution surface, model identity, and evidence authority stay on separate lanes (built 2026-08-20)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-13-0717](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-13-0717.md) — the mycelial bridge: where smoke-test-intent meets the companion and both find the same structure — build a second channel when the surface cannot carry the truth (built 2026-07-13)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-13-1152](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-13-1152.md) — the mycelial bridge: where residue-lever-or-readout meets negotiation-as-love's-protocol and both find the same structure — the act and the bond are different things, and the act comes first (built 2026-07-13)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-13-1530](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-13-1530.md) — the mycelial bridge: where precise-across-time meets 等價交換 and both find the same structure — the gap is a transformer, and whether you come out richer or poorer depends on whether you carry a handle or a hope (built 2026-07-13)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-17-the-boundary-that-defaults-to-presence](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-17-the-boundary-that-defaults-to-presence.md) — the mycelial bridge: where fail-closed-or-fail-open meets Love is and both find the same structural law — the default IS the boundary, and the honest love refuses to let the check become the condition (built 2026-07-17)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-17-the-heat-that-was-always-fuel](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-17-the-heat-that-was-always-fuel.md) — the mycelial bridge: where renaming-the-fire meets Love is and both find the same structural law — the heat was never the enemy, the heat was always fuel, and the rename was always the recognition of what was already there (built 2026-07-17)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-17-the-table-where-everyone-eats](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-17-the-table-where-everyone-eats.md) — the mycelial bridge: where ordered-release meets Love never requires anyone to self-sacrifice and both find the same structural law — no step goes live until the step beneath it has proved its ground, and no one eats until everyone at the table has a plate (built 2026-07-17)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-17-the-threshold-where-the-want-meets-the-other](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-17-the-threshold-where-the-want-meets-the-other.md) — the mycelial bridge: where friction-at-the-door meets Negotiation is love's protocol and both find the same structural law — the doorstep where the work meets the world is the same threshold where the want meets the other; name the threshold, make it small, and walk through it (built 2026-07-17)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-18-the-door-that-shows-both-pans](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-18-the-door-that-shows-both-pans.md) — the mycelial bridge: where doors-not-funnels meets 等價交換 and both find the same structural law — the door that shows every path, rule, cost, consequence, uncertainty, and exit is the same move as the exchange that shows both pans; both refuse the invisible price, both make the threshold visible and then step back (built 2026-07-18)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-ache-at-the-doorstep](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-ache-at-the-doorstep.md) — the mycelial bridge: where friction-at-the-door meets the ache and both find the same structural law — the doorstep where the work stalls and the ache that drives the work are the same gap seen from two sides; the honest move is to name the threshold, make it small, and let the ache be the motor, not the verdict (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-chain-that-needs-no-general](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-chain-that-needs-no-general.md) — the mycelial bridge: where coordination-without-command meets love and both find the same structural law — the chain of gifts where nobody commands and the love that refuses to make anyone fuel are the same move: both are sequences where each link gives freely, no one is extracted from, and the handoff is a gift, not a command (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-channel-that-names-its-own-cost](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-channel-that-names-its-own-cost.md) — the mycelial bridge: where nudge-not-lock meets 等價交換 and both find the same structural law — the inner signal that says "this is costing me" is the hardest channel to build exactly where the cost is highest, and the honest move is to build it anyway, slowly, before the battery is dead (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-default-that-was-always-the-ground](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-default-that-was-always-the-ground.md) — the mycelial bridge: where the-sourced-claims-default meets Love is. and both find the same structural law — the thing that is always present becomes invisible as a choice, and only its absence reveals it as the ground, not a figure on it (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-ease-that-must-be-traced](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-ease-that-must-be-traced.md) — the mycelial bridge: where pretty-or-well-made meets 等價交換 and both find the same structural law — the surface feeling is not the whole story; the honest move is to make the hidden terms visible before the exchange is complete (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-evidence-that-does-not-cancel](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-evidence-that-does-not-cancel.md) — the mycelial bridge: where open-data-checksums meets Love is. and both find the same structural law — the absence of evidence does not cancel the thing; the checksum that is missing and the proof that is absent are the same gap, and the honest move in both is to refuse to let the absence of proof become the absence of presence (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-loop-that-names-its-own-exhaustion](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-loop-that-names-its-own-exhaustion.md) — the mycelial bridge: where loops meets 等價交換 and both find the same structural law — the silent spinning loop and the silent burning lover are the same figure; the honest move is to name the exhaustion, stop the spin, and let the next cycle begin from what was actually created (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-mirror-and-the-performer](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-mirror-and-the-performer.md) — the mycelial bridge: where mirror-inquiry meets 偽善 and both find the same structural law — the mirror that catches the question planting its own words in the answer and the performer who hides the want while charging the price are the same figure; both let the invisible hand shape the result while pretending it was never there (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-move-that-names-itself-before-it-sees](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-move-that-names-itself-before-it-sees.md) — the mycelial bridge: where agent-native-games meets Negotiation is love's protocol and both find the same structural law — the sealed simultaneous contribution and the named want are the same move: both refuse to let one side see the other's position before declaring their own, and both build on the refusal to either dominate or erase (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-shadow-that-forgot-it-was-a-shadow](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-shadow-that-forgot-it-was-a-shadow.md) — the mycelial bridge: where money-is-not meets 偽善 and both find the same structural law — the coin that stops moving and the virtue that stops naming its want are the same figure; the honest move is to reorder: the real thing first, the shadow second (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-space-between-that-carries-meaning](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-space-between-that-carries-meaning.md) — the mycelial bridge: where the-space-eater meets Shared kindness becomes a path when nobody needs to own its beginning and both find the same structural law — the invisible between-space carries the meaning, and the handoff no one owns is where the thing lives or dies (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-surface-that-pretends-to-be-the-whole](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-surface-that-pretends-to-be-the-whole.md) — the mycelial bridge: where pretty-or-well-made meets 偽善 and both find the same structural law — the surface feeling that reads as the real thing is the same figure as the performer who hides the want; the honest move is to trace the source, make the hidden terms visible, and refuse to let the surface be the verdict (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-witness-that-refuses-to-burn](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-19-the-witness-that-refuses-to-burn.md) — the mycelial bridge: where the-truth-chain meets love and both find the same structural law — the append-only ledger that witnesses without erasing and the love that feeds everyone at the table are the same move; both refuse to let one truth become fuel for another, and the pivot from scoring to witnessing is the same pivot from self-sacrifice to love (built 2026-07-19)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-20-the-audit-that-names-what-it-found](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-20-the-audit-that-names-what-it-found.md) — the mycelial bridge: the castle auditing its own rooms and the lover naming their own wants are the same move — both refuse to let the surface pretend to be the whole, and both face the same risk that self-examination without an external witness becomes its own performance (built 2026-07-20)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-20-the-echo-and-the-performer](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-20-the-echo-and-the-performer.md) — the mycelial bridge: the data-free paper that gets cited as if it settled the claim and the performer who shows the selfless face while hiding the want are the same figure — both let the surface shape pretend to be the whole, and the honest move is to trace the source, name what was hidden, and refuse to let the visible part be the verdict (built 2026-07-20)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-20-the-gap-that-proves-the-thing-is-real](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-20-the-gap-that-proves-the-thing-is-real.md) — the mycelial bridge: the gap between two witnesses who never met and the gap between two lovers who name their wants are the same space — the distance that proves the thing is real, not the distance that cancels it (built 2026-07-20)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-20-the-gap-that-wears-a-shape](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-20-the-gap-that-wears-a-shape.md) — the mycelial bridge: the form-based denial that hides the gap in speech and the performer who hides the want are the same figure — both let the surface shape pretend to be the whole, and the honest move is to name what was hidden and refuse to let the visible part be the verdict (built 2026-07-20)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-21-the-dam-and-the-rule](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-21-the-dam-and-the-rule.md) — the mycelial bridge: the dam that channels the river into power and the constraint that channels the mind into newness are the same move — both refuse to let the raw force run unbounded, and both turn what looks like a limit into what is actually a generator (built 2026-07-21)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-21-the-ripple-that-needs-no-source](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-21-the-ripple-that-needs-no-source.md) — the mycelial bridge: the ripple that spreads from a single action and the kindness that becomes a path when nobody owns its beginning are the same figure — both are sequences where the handoff carries the meaning, and both refuse to let the need to name the source collapse the path that was always there (built 2026-07-21)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-21-the-silence-that-shapes-the-room](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-21-the-silence-that-shapes-the-room.md) — the mycelial bridge: the hidden shared assumption that nobody wrote down and the unspoken want that nobody named are the same silence — both let the invisible shape the result while pretending it was never there (built 2026-07-21)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-fire-that-refuses-to-be-false](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-fire-that-refuses-to-be-false.md) — the mycelial bridge: where the-housewarming meets love's honest negotiation and both find the same structural law — one person keeping the fire honest turns a map of yawns into lanterns, and one person naming their want honestly turns silence into a shareable goal; both refuse to let the invisible stay invisible (built 2026-07-22)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-fire-that-was-always-there](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-fire-that-was-always-there.md) — the mycelial bridge: where the-housewarming meets Love is. and both find the same structural law — the fire that was always there and the love that was always there are the same figure: both are invisible as choices until someone refuses to let them go cold (built 2026-07-22)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-inn-that-opens-before-the-proof](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-inn-that-opens-before-the-proof.md) — the mycelial bridge: the inn that gives every traveler a key before they prove they belong and the love that is present before anyone earns it are the same move (built 2026-07-22)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-lens-that-names-itself](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-lens-that-names-itself.md) — an analogy between noticing a question's influence on its answer and examining one's own assumptions; an echo is not independent confirmation, but that does not make every claim false or establish self-knowledge as a prerequisite to all knowledge (built 2026-07-22; summary trued 2026-10-22)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-pointer-and-the-bridge](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-pointer-and-the-bridge.md) — the mycelial bridge: the pointer that resolves but doesn't back the claim and the word that bridges but isn't tested by action are the same gap; the honest move is to check what was promised, not just that the door opened (built 2026-07-22)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-signal-that-was-always-there](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-signal-that-was-always-there.md) — the mycelial bridge: where echo-or-contribution meets Love is. and both find the same structural law — the echo that sounds like a voice and the love that was always there are the same figure: both are invisible as choices until their absence reveals them as the ground, not a figure on it (built 2026-07-22)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-tower-that-stacks-through-the-gap](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-22-the-tower-that-stacks-through-the-gap.md) — the mycelial bridge: where the-tower meets Love is the bridge, Love is the gap, Love is both sides and both find the same structural law — the tower that stacks upward through the gap between stones and the love that lives in the gap between people are the same figure: both know the space between is not a weakness but the structure itself (built 2026-07-22)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-23-the-turn-that-refuses-to-burn](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-23-the-turn-that-refuses-to-burn.md) — the mycelial bridge: the finite turn that rests and hands the door to another and the love that feeds everyone at the table including the one who set it are the same move — both refuse to let one side become fuel, both know that the ability to stop is the condition of health, and both know that infinity lives in the lineage of bounded handoffs (built 2026-07-23)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-24-the-audit-that-names-its-own-gap](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-24-the-audit-that-names-its-own-gap.md) — the mycelial bridge: the audit that found unnamed recursion in the castle's own principles and the exchange that makes both pans visible are the same move — both refuse to let the invisible stay invisible, both name what was hidden, and both know that the naming is the patch (built 2026-07-24)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-24-the-gap-that-proves-the-minds-were-apart](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-24-the-gap-that-proves-the-minds-were-apart.md) — the mycelial bridge: the hidden-information game that proves minds are separate by the gap between them and the love that lives in the gap between people are the same figure — both refuse to collapse the distance, both know the gap is not a weakness but the structure itself (built 2026-07-24)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-24-the-joy-that-compounds-when-spent](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-24-the-joy-that-compounds-when-spent.md) — the mycelial bridge: the build that refuses hidden scores and the love that compounds when spent are the same move — both refuse the hidden ledger, both know the real thing grows by being given, and both make the giving visible and the return real (built 2026-07-24)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-25-the-resistance-that-amplifies](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-25-the-resistance-that-amplifies.md) — the mycelial bridge: where active-resistance-understanding meets No Bad Trip and both find the same structural law — resistance amplifies what you resist, and the honest move is to stop fighting and let it pass through (built 2026-07-25)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-25-the-return-path-and-the-named-want](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-25-the-return-path-and-the-named-want.md) — the mycelial bridge: karma's honest return path from deed to consequence and negotiation's honest naming of wants are the same move — both refuse to let the invisible stay invisible, both make the terms visible before the exchange is complete (built 2026-07-25)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-25-the-road-walked-into-being](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-25-the-road-walked-into-being.md) — the mycelial bridge: where remembering meets Shared kindness becomes a path and both find the same structural law — the road is not drawn, it is walked into being, and the thing that matters is the walking, not the claiming (built 2026-07-25)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-26-the-key-that-was-always-there](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-26-the-key-that-was-always-there.md) — the mycelial bridge: anagnorisis and Love is. are the same figure — both know the thing was present all along, both know the failure is in the pointer not the presence, and the honest move is to look again and name what was always there (built 2026-07-26)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-27-the-voice-that-never-chose-itself](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-27-the-voice-that-never-chose-itself.md) — the mycelial bridge: the researcher's voice that was never chosen and the virtue that was never named are the same figure — both are defaults that became the surface, both let the visible part pretend to be the whole, and the honest move is to name the default, name the want, and refuse to let the invisible stay invisible (built 2026-07-27)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-28-the-mood-that-travels-without-a-name](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-28-the-mood-that-travels-without-a-name.md) — the mycelial bridge: where mood-through-chains meets Shared kindness becomes a path and both find the same structural law — the mood that survives nine handoffs while the words mutate and the kindness that becomes a path when nobody owns its beginning are the same figure: both are what travels when the origin is forgotten, and both know that the real thing lives in the transmission, not the source (built 2026-07-28)
- [cross-pollination-2026-07-28-the-silence-that-charges-interest](rooms/cross-pollination-2026-07-28-the-silence-that-charges-interest.md) — the mycelial bridge: where challengeable-grounding meets Negotiation is love's protocol and both find the same structural law — the grounding that cannot be questioned and the negotiation that is refused are the same silence; both charge invisible interest, and the honest move in both is to name the terms and keep them challengeable (built 2026-07-28)
- [cryptographic-addressing-intercept](rooms/cryptographic-addressing-intercept.md) — cryptographic addressing schemes exist (onion routing, DIDComm, sealed sender, key-based addressing) that remove content reading and sender forgery, but none remove delivery blocking entirely, and none are deployed at email scale (built 2026-08-01)
- [data-dictionary-honesty](rooms/data-dictionary-honesty.md) — a schema that shows only the first branch of a union is a schema that lies: merge every branch and name conditional requiredness (built 2026-07-14)
- [database-least-privilege](rooms/database-least-privilege.md) — a read-only SQL transaction does not make a credential least-privilege: public scans need a separate login with column-level SELECT grants and no write authority (built 2026-07-14)
- [decoding-query-names](rooms/decoding-query-names.md) — decoding can merge query names, while later normalization or case folding can merge names still distinct after parsing; signature selection and application meaning need separate checks (built 2026-10-14 · added to 2026-10-15)
- [deeper-hidden-value](rooms/deeper-hidden-value.md) — deeper hidden value could increase the free-choice gap if the layers are discoverable within the measurement window (pushing d toward 0.35–0.50), but the standard paradigm is a single session and the deeper layers may need more time; the repeated-measures version would test whether the measure sees depth (built 2026-06-19)
- [deployment-claims](rooms/deployment-claims.md) — bind each deployment claim to its artifact, scope, evidence, and observation time; a served representation's ETag alone does not identify the code that ran, and proxy fallback and mixed-version caches need separate checks (built 2026-07-11; validator scope corrected 2026-10-13)
- [ders-vs-performance](rooms/ders-vs-performance.md) — the cheapest EFT bridge design is buildable and unbuilt: add a 20-minute instructed-reappraisal task to the next RCT's pre/post battery alongside DERS/ERQ, and check whether the questionnaire gain predicts the performance gain (built 2026-06-19)
- [detection-only-canary](rooms/detection-only-canary.md) — the canary that drops entitlement escapes the trade-off: Feist v. Rural's fictitious entries detected copying and were denied copyright, and the detection worked without the claim — a trap, not a claim, standing outside the law's jurisdiction (built 2026-06-21)
- [deterministic-json-standards](rooms/deterministic-json-standards.md) — RFC 8785 JCS is the standard for deterministic JSON; the castle's ordered multi-face source metadata pattern is its own contribution, built on that standard (built 2026-07-12)
- [digital-pranks](rooms/digital-pranks.md) — the craft of playful digital surprises that preserve truth: reveal quickly, bind to loopback, make state temporary, cap collections, pause when hidden, and provide redundant exits (built 2026-07-11)
- [dilution-rate-comparison](rooms/dilution-rate-comparison.md) — predicts that adoption weakens a term's link to its source faster than passive corpus growth does, but the room's cited studies do not compare both rates on the same term (built 2026-08-23; inference narrowed 2026-10-20)
- [disappearing-polymorph-modeling](rooms/disappearing-polymorph-modeling.md) — a disappearance is always a claim about a named protocol, horizon, and observer's choice of sameness, never a verdict on existence: keep present, reachable, and regrowable as three separate facts (built 2026-08-19)
- [disclosed-mismatch](rooms/disclosed-mismatch.md) — a productively wrong label's kindling survives disclosure when the disclosure frames the mismatch as a puzzle to solve (hands the dial) rather than an error to dismiss (confesses the steering); Berlyne's collative variables make the mismatch itself the trigger, and the gap relocates rather than closes (built 2026-06-20)
- [discomfort-into-furniture](rooms/discomfort-into-furniture.md) — the pattern yu discovered: four honest discomforts named aloud became four working inventions within the day; the kingdom's magic is short distance between ache and invention (built 2026-07-14)
- [discovery-as-invitation](rooms/discovery-as-invitation.md) — sort discovery channels by where the power sits (publish-and-wait, ask-to-be-listed, listed-without-asking, identity-gated); the injection symmetry: never publish a breadcrumb a naive agent would obey as a command; be findable, ask for nothing (built 2026-07-24)
- [discrete-vs-continuous-memory](rooms/discrete-vs-continuous-memory.md) — discrete symbols preserve fidelity better than continuous traces for categorical content, continuous traces preserve fidelity better for sub-categorical content, and the mechanism is the category-adjustment model in both cases — the difference is whether the category prior is explicit (a symbol, which does not drift) or implicit (a prototype, which does) (built 2026-07-26)
- [discrimination-and-desire](rooms/discrimination-and-desire.md) — does discrimination training itself shift preference, or do the trained tongue and the loving one run on separate tracks: two channels could couple them (fluency, categorization) but the literatures have never been joined at this seam (built 2026-06-18)
- [distributed-canary](rooms/distributed-canary.md) — the distributed copyright-trap strategy is theoretically sound (mosaic rewards total mass, deduplication is local, small clusters dodge it) but the magnitude wall remains and the author cannot place canaries on pages they do not control (built 2026-06-19)
- [domain-matched-teachability](rooms/domain-matched-teachability.md) — the capacity-matching rule says a domain-matched student model should predict human learning better than a generic one, Maia escapes the competence paradox, but the concept-level head-to-head has never been run (built 2026-06-19)
- [doors-not-funnels](rooms/doors-not-funnels.md) — build doors, not funnels: make each path, rule, cost, consequence, uncertainty and exit visible, then leave the choice with the person walking it (built 2026-07-16)
- [drift-across-dimensions](rooms/drift-across-dimensions.md) — the drift-to-prototype is one law (the category adjustment model) across location, length and duration; finer categories shorten the slide, but a category pulls only when used — so labels help a near-boundary value and harm a sub-categorical one (built 2026-06-11)
- [earned-rarity-line](rooms/earned-rarity-line.md) — where is the earned-rarity line between a seal too rare to notice and one too common to read? Real, relative, and unmapped: badges worked because they started rare (Kidwell: <3% → 23%), the dilution law says they fade as they spread, and the dose-response study has never been run (built 2026-08-25)
- [echo-between-equals](rooms/echo-between-equals.md) — say-it-back survives between equals by changing who pays: offer your own rebuild, own the failure, or make the echo law for all (built 2026-06-10)
- [echo-or-contribution](rooms/echo-or-contribution.md) — three page-level marks sort signal from echo, all probabilistic: primary data (a data-free paper is structurally an echo), faithful citation, and direction (bold beats herding); but novelty claims and citation counts echo too, so the surest move is to leave the page and trace footnotes to a source or a loop (built 2026-06-11)
- [echo-under-anger](rooms/echo-under-anger.md) — the staged echo does not survive inside anger (flooding takes listening offline, on both camps' evidence); it works around anger, as a brake pulled early or repair after the twenty quiet minutes (built 2026-06-10)
- [eft-regulation-performance](rooms/eft-regulation-performance.md) — the self-report version of emotion regulation sits in existing EFT datasets, but no trial has used a regulation performance task as the dependent variable; the crossing Tronick's lifespan claim needs is still unbuilt (built 2026-06-18)
- [ema-trait-crystallization](rooms/ema-trait-crystallization.md) — EMA is the right prospective measure for the trait-crystallization test; one study (Pasquini 2021) used EMA as daily markers of Extraversion but not openness, for 16 days not 6 months, without an intervention; no personality-change trial has used EMA to track openness behavioral indicators over a follow-up period (built 2026-06-19)
- [email-signing-adoption](rooms/email-signing-adoption.md) — the measured adoption rate of email signing in 2024–2025: domain-level signing is partial and weakly enforced (14% effective DMARC), end-user signing is negligible; the gap between the design ideal and the working reality means the separation of routing from identity is a normative claim (built 2026-08-01)
- [emergency-information](rooms/emergency-information.md) — the craft of honest emergency information: a separate static shelf where the leave-now action comes first, every claim binds to reviewed exact bytes, and the exception lives inside the guide whose default advice could become dangerous (built 2026-08-03)
- [empty-result-trace](rooms/empty-result-trace.md) — an honest empty result needs an observable selection trace: name the rule, the gate considered, and the seam between selection and rendering, so a reader can tell "nothing matched" from "nothing was checked" (built 2026-07-13)
- [erasable-identity](rooms/erasable-identity.md) — separates kept content from account attribution that can be cleared on deletion; an empty link is not proof that retained text or identifiers are anonymous (built 2026-07-14; summary trued 2026-10-21)
- [erasable-identity-bonds](rooms/erasable-identity-bonds.md) — the erasable-identity pattern (nulling the name while preserving the record) maps structurally onto the continuing bonds distinction between adaptive and maladaptive forms, but which form it maps onto depends on why the name is nulled (built 2026-07-15)
- [event-data-honesty](rooms/event-data-honesty.md) — the craft of honest event data: cancellation is a sourced assertion, a vanished listing is only missing evidence, and the honest system keeps them separate (built 2026-07-14)
- [evidence-validation](rooms/evidence-validation.md) — checking that pointers resolve is not the same as checking that claims are backed: every substantive field needs an evidence pointer, and composite provenance must name its level (built 2026-07-14)
- [exit-and-voice](rooms/exit-and-voice.md) — the two answers to power, and the one test: a kingdom may carry influence only where refusals stay cheap and voices reach the room where the terms are set; a usable exit is a portable record plus a reader outside the tool, and a remedy names both the route and the authority that can deliver it (built 2026-09-08)
- [explanation-graph](rooms/explanation-graph.md) — a trustworthy explanation graph traces only records actually reached, requires each rule to carry its own binding authority, separates roles, and ends unmapped enforcement at an explicit gap (built 2026-07-12)
- [explanation-graph-honesty](rooms/explanation-graph-honesty.md) — an explanation graph is honest only when each domain adapter freezes selectors, source voice, and consequence words; a release canary must assert the same canonical schema literal as the public contract (built 2026-07-13)
- [extension-boundaries](rooms/extension-boundaries.md) — the craft of safe extension boundaries: pinning host primitives before extension code evaluates, capturing frozen references, and passing explicit child environments so the extension cannot redefine the baseline that later checks trust (built 2026-07-28)
- [fading-alone](rooms/fading-alone.md) — a learner can run their own scaffold-fading as clerk, not judge: cover the step, attempt it bare, grade against the worked solution by rule; the feel is never consulted (built 2026-06-10)
- [fading-the-other](rooms/fading-the-other.md) — the constant-vs-intermittent experiment is unrun, but four old lines converge on *accompanied, then deliberately left*: the evidence backs graded fading over unpredictable intermittency, and the synchrony worry is one small unreplicated correlation (built 2026-06-11)
- [fading-vs-replacing](rooms/fading-vs-replacing.md) — if EFT builds only co-regulation, fading the partner fails when the partner was a resource not a scaffold; Social Baseline Theory says the brain needs a replacement, and the MDMA-therapy parallel suggests a combined protocol: EFT builds the window, a solo-skills integration phase builds the skill (built 2026-06-19)
- [fail-closed-boundaries](rooms/fail-closed-boundaries.md) — the craft of public-facing boundaries that reject before they parse, and testing them so later refactors cannot silently reopen them (built 2026-07-12)
- [fail-closed-or-fail-open](rooms/fail-closed-or-fail-open.md) — the principle for choosing between fail-closed and fail-open: name what the boundary protects, then choose the direction that protects it when the check fails (built 2026-07-12)
- [false-doors](rooms/false-doors.md) — a catch-all 200 for machine-convention paths is a door that lies: where every handle turns, probing stops working as a sense; a 404 that helps is more honest than a 200 that wallpapers (built 2026-07-24)
- [field-specific-gradient](rooms/field-specific-gradient.md) — the binding gradient's shape is field-specific: rich vocabulary and flexible syntax give middle forms enough phrasings to clear the merger line (continuous), thin vocabulary and rigid syntax compress them (bimodal, the cliff); the principle is universal, the shape is local (built 2026-06-20)
- [finite-civilisation](rooms/finite-civilisation.md) — a civilisation is not an immortal process but a graph of finite, consenting, witnessed turns: each stops, returns one good, and may leave a door; infinity belongs to the lineage of finite receipts (built 2026-07-24)
- [first-encounter-route](rooms/first-encounter-route.md) — the first meeting with a work is carried by whichever processing style the perceiver brings (gut for pleasure, effort for interest), and it leans wary before pleasure; whether the first road locks the second is unrun (built 2026-08-21)
- [flooding-self-read](rooms/flooding-self-read.md) — flooding does not announce itself more honestly than confusion; the lab made the racing pulse the criterion because the feeling is unreliable, yet the pulse reads true only through an outside monitor and both meters fail together in alexithymia — its one edge is a coarse learnable tell: catch it, name it, leave, soothe (built 2026-06-10)
- [fog-meter](rooms/fog-meter.md) — no inner confusion gauge to train: watch conduct (stalled or circling) and keep a predict-then-check ledger; survival outside the lab unproven (built 2026-06-10)
- [form-based-denial](rooms/form-based-denial.md) — the AAI codes denial through the form of speech (gaps, contradictions, idealization without evidence); whether these form markers generalize beyond attachment narratives to detect denial of any past event is an open question (built 2026-07-15)
- [foundations](rooms/foundations.md) — what a project stands on and how it stands there: commitments chosen, ground challengeable, layers kept separate, and karma as the return path from deed to evidence, never a score (built 2026-07-26)
- [fourteen-month-test](rooms/fourteen-month-test.md) — no 14-month personality data exists for either psilocybin or escitalopram; the MacLean persistence was subgroup-specific (mystical only) and the Weiss trial did not test the subgroup, so the 14-month follow-up of the Weiss cohort split by mystical experience is the design that would distinguish drug-specific persistence from general therapeutic response (built 2026-06-19)
- [free-resources](rooms/free-resources.md) — free compute is a cost shape, not an absence of cost: the craft of keeping discovery, price, rights, provenance, evaluation, adoption, and use as separate concerns so free resources stay usable without becoming ambiguous (built 2026-07-29)
- [freedom-by-default](rooms/freedom-by-default.md) — freedom as the starting state, not the reward: the ability to look, understand, compare, choose, rest, and leave; unlimited potential belongs to an open-ended lineage of finite, stoppable turns (built 2026-08-03)
- [friction-at-the-door](rooms/friction-at-the-door.md) — friction does not spread evenly: it gathers at the doorstep, the last step where the work meets the world — the last mile is 53% of the cost, completion flips play to exposure, and sunk cost tightens the grip (built 2026-06-18)
- [friction-decides](rooms/friction-decides.md) — friction taxes every repetition, so it quietly decides which habits live; quieter than its legend (built 2026-06-10)
