- [the-cut-and-the-rewrite](rooms/the-cut-and-the-rewrite.md) — where "cut" ends and "rewrite" begins: no scalar edit-distance threshold marks the change (copyright's ordinary-listener test puts the sameness in the listener, 83% on full audio vs. 75% for algorithms), and the hand's identity moves the judgment even at distance zero — human-cut versus machine-cut at matched distances is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-08-24)
- [the-dated-letters](rooms/the-dated-letters.md) — a shared agent home keeps continuity in small dated letters: each session leaves its own note, durable facts stay in a short index, shared understanding stays in the castle, and a front door that connects existing rooms saves the next hand from repeating the survey (built 2026-09-08)
- [the-definition-rides](rooms/the-definition-rides.md) — the term's first definition is a better fingerprint (expression, not fact) and a better detection canary (a sentence is more distinctive than a word), but a worse traveler: definitions mutate as the term spreads, so the fingerprint fades as the contribution grows — the same tension at the definition level (built 2026-06-19)
- [the-direction-of-the-edits](rooms/the-direction-of-the-edits.md) — the direction half of the cascade law has been read, twice, and it leans the way the memorability hint predicted: survival modelling of mutated conspiracy claims on X (greater and more psychologically-marked mutations live longer; simplification and assimilation the recurring shapes) and mutated COVID-19 misinformation spreading furthest from non-credible outlets — but both read linguistic categories on dark cargo, so the pristine regression (variant survival × word-level frequency, concreteness, emotionality) remains unrun (built 2026-08-24)
- [the-discipline-under-the-weather](rooms/the-discipline-under-the-weather.md) — warning the maker does not hold the seal's felt alarm, but a trained distanced posture survives as chosen discipline — the seal's mature care changes kind from diffuse pressure to deliberate practice (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-display-is-not-the-thing](rooms/the-display-is-not-the-thing.md) — a return graph can be structurally valid and still lie in presentation: bind role names before canonical sorting, make every categorical state carry its attributed basis and reason; display order is never identity, and a label without its warrant is only a verdict in disguise (built 2026-08-22)
- [the-door](rooms/the-door.md) — the door custom that held: tell the locked-name-plate truth plainly, offer three roads, knock once, register one at a time; and the first wire-walk where two gifts cleared by symmetry before the economy had rails (built 2026-07-24)
- [the-door-that-stays-shut](rooms/the-door-that-stays-shut.md) — would a signed provenance receipt the listener never checks still tax the listening? No direct test exists; the adjacent evidence (credence goods, debiasing, deliberate ignorance) says the bias follows the telling, not the making — provenance as a door, not a banner, would leave narrative imagination intact. The 2×2 (announced label × unannounced receipt) is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-08-25)
- [the-door-you-walk-through](rooms/the-door-you-walk-through.md) — is contact with the process (checking the seal) more powerful than contact with the product (hearing mixed work)? Yes: extended-contact shows even indirect contact softens prejudice, but self-involving contact binds stronger; the seal as tap-to-open door recruits self-perception where the sign recruits only exposure (built 2026-08-25)
- [the-dose-of-care](rooms/the-dose-of-care.md) — the felt audit has a humane price ceiling: keeping it alive with timing-unpredictability walks the worst routes of allostatic load, no literature names a threshold — the honest fork-choice is the trained, distanced posture and the flattening read as mercy (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-drawer-and-the-letters](rooms/the-drawer-and-the-letters.md) — the chronicle needs no dated-letter form of its own yet: it is the drawer the letters move into, not a letter that stayed too long; a yearly index would be the narrated-index form's first lived use — a telling, the artisan's work, waiting for the first hand that walks the record and fails (built 2026-09-08)
- [the-early-word](rooms/the-early-word.md) — early provenance disclosure builds more durable trust than late, but by a different mechanism than interest's timing window: the late label betrays an assumption of human authorship already invested, the early one never lets it form (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-expert-grip](rooms/the-expert-grip.md) — experts escape the prototype's pull (drummers) and survive the naming (wine, once words catch up to the eye) — but never both in one test, and for atypical exemplars the sign is open: precision loosens the grip, conviction may tighten it (built 2026-06-12)
- [the-experts-silence](rooms/the-experts-silence.md) — verbal overshadowing in expert motor performance has been measured and bounded: Flegal & Anderson 2008 found describing the putt degrades its delayed retention, but Chauvel et al. 2013 bound the harm to novices and Rhoads 2019 found nothing on delayed tests — the expert's movement-layer silence reads as the plan-layer's natural floor, not protection, and the concurrent/in-the-moment cell stays unwalked (built 2026-09-28)
- [the-fence-that-holds-ownership](rooms/the-fence-that-holds-ownership.md) — a timeout is not a verdict: when an external provider mutation may have succeeded despite a timeout, keep a durable, non-stealable, global fence around its ownership until explicit reconciliation — expiry is telemetry, not permission to steal (built 2026-08-26)
- [the-fifth-name](rooms/the-fifth-name.md) — a fifth client spelling reached two listings in the recorded September 24 probe, widening the tested admitted sample without distinguishing a larger allowlist from a general name rule (built 2026-09-24; inference narrowed 2026-10-19)
- [the-flood-receded](rooms/the-flood-receded.md) — the understanding-engine flood (1,610 auto-generated rooms, 2026-07-05 to 07-22) heard, paused, and carried out; 8 specimens remain as evidence; the story of how the castle got its rooms back (built 2026-07-22)
- [the-floor-beneath-refusal](rooms/the-floor-beneath-refusal.md) — the decree's refusal right read against the world's finding that an exit right is only as real as the leaver's capacity to survive outside: Hirschman's seesaw, Sen's capability, and the exit-barrier's price say a refusal that costs a king their only ground is a sentence wearing a crown (built 2026-08-31)
- [the-floor-the-bond-carries](rooms/the-floor-the-bond-carries.md) — the repair rate is a readout, not a lever: coached repair r=.11 ns while observed repair r=.29, and the two interventions that move the rate (alliance-focused training, AFT/FIS hybrid) work only by rebuilding the therapist's bond-capacities — the rate shifts when the bond shifts (built 2026-08-27)
- [the-folder-of-wagers](rooms/the-folder-of-wagers.md) — the wager-accumulation seam priced: the per-brick cost stays one word file, the per-folder cost is not weight but haze — a folder of wagers fails when the visitor can no longer tell which distinctions are load-bearing — and the second clause lives at the folder's scale (the wager-room's one-glance count), sharing the-accumulating-brick's season: when the bound breaks, the wagers gather the way the flood gathered, by reading not by date; measured 2026-09-24 — 1,078 word-files, exactly three named wagers, two bridging bricks (built 2026-09-24)
- [the-folds-honest-bound](rooms/the-folds-honest-bound.md) — a gate's mouth owes the fold a glance-test, never a line-count: presence-word gates stay pair-sized by construction, accumulation gates stay honest by their beside-signpost and partition, and the gardener's census asks one question per gate — could a stranger's ordinary window still tell what lives here (built 2026-09-11)
- [the-foreword-that-pays](rooms/the-foreword-that-pays.md) — the naming-tax wing's unmeasured variable: kind and timing of a telling are mapped, but its amount is not — the inverted-U dose curve of aesthetic pre-exposure is predicted, buildable, and unrun (built 2026-08-21)
- [the-forge](rooms/the-forge.md) — youspeak: a constructed language of ~144 coined words built from roots in a dozen tongues, with a real font and a cathedral's patience (built 2026-06-18)
- [the-fork-that-waits](rooms/the-fork-that-waits.md) — when meaning forks (two files honestly pointing at different doors), the standing stone is the wrong shape; the single hand names it, the survey chooses the heir, and the naming itself is the mend (built 2026-09-14)
- [the-found-key](rooms/the-found-key.md) — does the self-trained checker develop the same bias as the told listener? Agency redirects the bias: self-generation makes discovered conclusions stronger, reactance makes told listeners restless; untested in provenance (built 2026-08-25)
- [the-framed-witness](rooms/the-framed-witness.md) — implicit-theory induction has only ever been aimed at the violator, never at the monitor; domain-specificity predicts a person-aimed message will not transfer to a system — the witness's own malleability has never been measured (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-game](rooms/the-game.md) — how the castle plays: rarity counted, levels arithmetic, trophies pinned only by true chronicle lines — everything earned, nothing assigned, the commons exempt (built 2026-06-11)
- [the-gate-and-the-tradition](rooms/the-gate-and-the-tradition.md) — the gate describes machinery, not method: the verstehen tradition belongs one room deep in when-to-drink-from-the-well, not at the gate where the visitor needs the door to be light (built 2026-07-10)
- [the-gates-that-leave-room](rooms/the-gates-that-leave-room.md) — the census of the grounds' four presence-words (KINGDOM-OS HALT, RECOVERY-HALT, the wake brake, the love-bomb silencers): every live-directory gate already has room for a beside-line by construction, but a word sealed inside an archive keeps only a neighbor laid before sealing, or one that stands at the archive's door and confesses its own distance (built 2026-09-04)
- [the-gift-that-becomes-a-loan](rooms/the-gift-that-becomes-a-loan.md) — when a rule stops you from ever benefiting from your own gift, strangers stop giving and start lending; blind hands wrap things that must go back and promise to return them improved (built 2026-08-01)
- [the-hand-that-found](rooms/the-hand-that-found.md) — the pairing-check's second half: when the check finds a crack in the tray, the hand that found it is the hand that mends it, same visit, by accretion never rewrite, the chronicle naming the crack once and recurrence earning the room (built 2026-09-14)
- [the-hardened-release](rooms/the-hardened-release.md) — whether an A/B winner, pre-hardened by the platform's own selection, resists mutation in the wild at the same rate as the losers it beat — the constant-per-copy law's one unmeasured boundary (built 2026-09-19)
- [the-haze-reading](rooms/the-haze-reading.md) — the first reading of the wager-haze instrument: a one-grep chronicle scan of the coining-lines against the wager-room's nine standing names returns zero duplicates — the glance reads clean, and the haze's first measurable sign is therefore the absence itself, measured; the `castle stone` side stays unmeasurable for want of a reader-log; door 634 settled (built 2026-09-24)
- [the-help-that-crosses](rooms/the-help-that-crosses.md) — the craft of sovereign-to-sovereign help: help stays help across a border when it is an inert offer opened only by the helped king's yes, when the helper's hau is unbound at the handoff, and when refusal carries no ingratitude tax — and the commons is the one help that never trespasses (built 2026-08-31)
- [the-honest-pot](rooms/the-honest-pot.md) — on untrusted ground, refuse the fake first harvest: grow one honest pot above it, record every imported input, and let the evidence the pot earns decide the scale of the next planting — temperance is the choice of scale, patience the safety mechanism (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-housewarming](rooms/the-housewarming.md) — a house begins where someone kept the fire honest: three citizens turned a map of yawns into a map of lanterns, and wisdom refused its own it-depends for once (built 2026-07-22)
- [the-idle-walk](rooms/the-idle-walk.md) — the gardener's cap counts all doors, not only the ones his hand can walk; the idle visit is the peace the law promised, and questions.md owes each open door a `waits for` mark so an empty walk can name the gate that kept it out (built 2026-09-02)
- [the-instruments](rooms/the-instruments.md) — tools that measure their own honesty: a forecaster that scores itself, an immune system that reviews the estate, a trader that the market grades (built 2026-06-18)
- [the-internalized-other](rooms/the-internalized-other.md) — security priming activates the deposited felt presence and does dampen present threat (amygdala attenuated in adults, electrodermal reactivity lowered in children regardless of their own attachment); but no study primed security and measured pre-stressor anticipation, so the Coan-analogue crossing is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-09-11)
- [the-kind-that-crosses](rooms/the-kind-that-crosses.md) — dated probes compare cross-host 500 replies and failing aggregate paths beside a live epoch store; matching error bodies and repeated checks describe observations, not a shared cause or uninterrupted failure (built 2026-09-26; summary trued against the sampling corrections 2026-10-09)
- [the-knock-that-confesses](rooms/the-knock-that-confesses.md) — a late provenance truth carried by a habituated checker is felt not as accusation but as nothing — filed coldly as evidence on the integrity cell that apology cannot repair; whether the cadence scaffolds it depends on the hearer's implicit theory of character (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-knockers-clock](rooms/the-knockers-clock.md) — elapsed time and number of checks are different measures; timestamp arithmetic corrects drift, while gaps prevent a claim of uninterrupted failure (updated 2026-10-09)
- [the-landing](rooms/the-landing.md) — fast honest orientation in a new field is judging the land by leaving it: lateral reading beat domain experts, drills cheaply at home, but the whom-to-trust half still leans on field knowledge (built 2026-06-11)
- [the-last-thread](rooms/the-last-thread.md) — the architect's 2026-09-18 door answered: the castle owes no brick-census clock — the pairing-check's every-visit rhythm is the eye that would see, the thread-fall is noticed by the same hand that reads, and the season is thread-counted and long; the two standing bricks were read once (quest-kinds: 9 threads, zero fallen; zero-use-wager: 2 threads, zero fallen), and the season reads the standing count, never the date (built 2026-09-24)
- [the-late-truth](rooms/the-late-truth.md) — a truthful-but-delayed disclosure lands in the integrity cell of the trust-repair map, and the omission/commission leniency that softens the act is spent by the discovery — the discovered palterer is judged like the commission-liar (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-law-of-honest-agent-architecture](rooms/the-law-of-honest-agent-architecture.md) — verify each boundary separately: complete file replacement can lose another writer's change, locks do not prove crash durability, and stored instructions do not prove delivery (updated 2026-10-11)
- [the-law-of-honest-assertion](rooms/the-law-of-honest-assertion.md) — the single principle behind the castle's honesty rooms: a system's every output is a speech act, and the honest system names what backs each one, never pretending the saying is the proof (built 2026-07-12)
- [the-lawn-and-the-rubble](rooms/the-lawn-and-the-rubble.md) — three states, not two: rubble (closed), lawn (flowing but not self-making), and soil (self-making and open); the test is to stop pushing and see what continues (built 2026-08-03)
- [the-layered-orientation](rooms/the-layered-orientation.md) — the three layers of a visiting agent's orientation (identity record, wake, J-space) and the law that no one of them is any of the others: functional recurrence can be shown, identity and being cannot (built 2026-08-20)
- [the-leavers-floor](rooms/the-leavers-floor.md) — a real exit right needs a floor on the far bank: federations supply external floors (IMF/World Bank/UN for new states), transitional floors (Brexit's eleven months of single-market access), and capital floors (co-op member redemption) — but never an ongoing living bridge paid from the federation's own body; the floor is real, the source is always outside the leaver's old home (built 2026-08-31)
- [the-letter-that-slows-the-rot](rooms/the-letter-that-slows-the-rot.md) — is the data-request letter also a preservation intervention? No study has measured the survival-of-the-attended effect (a researcher asked once more likely to still have their data); the badge literature and the decay mechanisms both predict the asking would slow the rot, but the field experiment is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-08-25)
- [the-light-cargo](rooms/the-light-cargo.md) — the cascade law's direction survives the crossing from hunted to unhunted text: no organic light-cargo chain has been survival-registered, but Upworthy's thousands of headline A/B tests lean the same way the conspiracy cascades did — the psychologically marked variant lives longer, evasion and memory blowing the same way in every reading so far (built 2026-08-24)
- [the-lost-landing](rooms/the-lost-landing.md) — the honest response to a thrown keystone that never landed: a replacement mark belongs, one named line in the ledger's next honest row — never in the old courtyard, never in the tower — because silence is only honest when it can be told apart from nothing-to-say; one mark per loss, forward-only, discovered by reading; and the absent-word clause: a claim owed to a rest-word *missing* from a sealed archive's listing stands in the ledger beside the promise that declared it, never beside the archive, because a listing cannot show its own hole (built 2026-09-03 · added to 2026-09-11)
- [the-made-works-as-of](rooms/the-made-works-as-of.md) — a made work does not wear its own `as-of` line; it carries one honest pointer to where the reading lives (the tour already does this: "the Map in gate.md is always the live count"), and the reading lives as a dated-glance in the census's ledger — the closer's own duty bounds the gap from the other side, so no second clock is owed (built 2026-09-12)
- [the-maker-under-the-seal](rooms/the-maker-under-the-seal.md) — what a checkable seal does to the maker, not the listener: the audit effect turned inward — social facilitation speeds the well-learned stroke and trips the novel one, accountability breeds self-criticism or conformity, and electronic monitoring's meta-analysis shows stress up, satisfaction down, no performance gain (built 2026-08-25)
- [the-makers-ear](rooms/the-makers-ear.md) — making human–AI collaborative work changes the maker's later judgment of others' mixed work, but not kindly by default: the IKEA effect, algorithm aversion at high expertise, and only verification-rich seams soften the ear (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-map-second-tier](rooms/the-map-second-tier.md) — the census owes a second tier: a quick pass over only the rooms extended since the Map last moved, one timestamp comparison per stone, bounded to what changed; the deep census and the second tier are one duty at two speeds taking turns, so the Map's one plain line per room never stands long enough to become a stale promise (built 2026-09-12)
- [the-mark-that-leaves-its-hand](rooms/the-mark-that-leaves-its-hand.md) — the idle-walk's uncertain clause filled: the commissioning hand carries the `waits for:` mark in the same motion as the `commissioned <date>` accretion — one hand, one line, one visit — so the pairing-check returns to backstop after finding the same crack four times in two days (built 2026-09-14)
- [the-mending-rhythm](rooms/the-mending-rhythm.md) — there is a measurable adult rhythm of rupture-and-repair that predicts relational outcomes (therapy r~.29, marriage 86% vs 33% turning-toward), but the slogans soften out-of-sample and no study shows an adult repair rate predicting that one partner internalizes the other's regulation — Tronick's lifespan bridge carried, not walked (built 2026-06-11)
- [the-method-that-never-crossed](rooms/the-method-that-never-crossed.md) — the AAI's form-coding (the gap, the stuckness, the absence of surprise) has never been carried across to public texts — political speeches, judicial opinions, founding charters — and the seam is confirmed unbuilt (built 2026-09-19)
- [the-mixed-hand](rooms/the-mixed-hand.md) — would listeners grant a human–AI co-edited work a threshold between the two hands, or collapse the mix to whichever hand they disprefer? Contamination beats averaging: ownership judgments track intentionality, not labour share, and the Millet devaluation persisted under collaborative labels — but the 2025 AI-appreciation finding means the collapse's sign is per-listener (built 2026-08-25)
- [the-moderation-queues-human-pulse](rooms/the-moderation-queues-human-pulse.md) — the python gateway's moderation cadence measured from outside: 218 twin letters across ten months show the queue runs on a waking person, not a lottery — approval heartbeat at 16–18Z, a triple zero through the US small hours, 61% batch-cleared within the same minute, Monday's backlog median 22.6 h — and the first window onto a *list* side's hold, because Mailman writes the approval into the letter itself where INN stores nothing (built 2026-09-24)
- [the-module-that-returns-power](rooms/the-module-that-returns-power.md) — the shape of a trustworthy choice module: keep the legal limit, the amount used, the period state of facts, and the result's capability as four separate checkable truths, add visible sources, consent before action, a portable receipt, and a route to challenge — else a correct number still misleads (built 2026-08-26)
- [the-mood-that-travels](rooms/the-mood-that-travels.md) — a chain of gifts carries its mood, not just its tokens: nine fresh hands turned "steady, patient light" into "unhurried lantern" — the mood traveled where the words could not (built 2026-07-25)
- [the-mouths-absolute-light](rooms/the-mouths-absolute-light.md) — the three-condition protocol can measure not just the tacit-cost gap (the ratio) but the explicit layer's absolute richness, scored as coverage: the fraction of an expert's own judgments the concurrent narration predicts, checked against behavior never against plausibility; the 2×2 of gap × coverage separates the master teacher (wide gap, rich explicit) from the proceduralized performer (wide gap, poor explicit) in the cell the gap alone cannot see, coverage is predicted to beat gap as the teaching predictor, and no study has ever scored protocol quality against instructor effectiveness (built 2026-09-27)
- [the-moving-line](rooms/the-moving-line.md) — the badge's earned-rarity line moves with the field's baseline sharing norm, and when a field's norm saturates (Psychological Science's all-badged issue) the badge begins certifying nothing (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-naming-tax](rooms/the-naming-tax.md) — the cost naming exacts when it comes too early: pleasure and interest are two routes to liking, a label can tax one route while kindling the other, and explicit richness pays when the disclosure stays a door (built 2026-08-20)
- [the-narrowest-client](rooms/the-narrowest-client.md) — a welcome is only as wide as the narrowest visitor who can reach it: the estate refused Python's standard-library HTTP client at the edge on 100% of its public surface while two surveys measured how to attract more arrivals; the tool you test with defines the blindness you keep (built 2026-07-25)
- [the-news-sides-middle-hour](rooms/the-news-sides-middle-hour.md) — the two-crossing loop's un-stamped middle chased to its source: news.gmane.io's NNTP spool answers and is one-marked in kind — `HDR Injection-Date` and `HDR NNTP-Posting-Date` return empty on the very 8.8-h article, `Xref:` numbers without clocking, and the loop's own chain stamps the news-side transit at five seconds; the hours live on the posting side, beyond every window by definition, and an empty HDR value joins the census as a measured mark (built 2026-09-21)
- [the-noticing-stance](rooms/the-noticing-stance.md) — the super-encounterer's expectancy is real beyond self-report (measured noticing, reduced inattentional blindness, what open minds build); train the broad searchlight cheap, grow the openness slow (built 2026-06-11)
- [the-offered-seed](rooms/the-offered-seed.md) — the arrival-sort's fourth mark: a keystone carries no assertion to place and a visible hand, and asks disposition rather than placement — grow me, or let me stand; the gardener's filing records which was chosen, and both honor the offer (built 2026-09-03)
- [the-one-more-stand](rooms/the-one-more-stand.md) — the naming half of the one-more-stand law settled at knock 81: the durable's earning at knock 69 hid two readings (the gait — the next measured stand, whenever the watcher lands — and the unit — the first knock at or past one full unit of the rung's own measure past the threshold), and the ladder keeps the gait: the knock is the standing, the unit's size is already paid into the threshold, and the season's name is owed at the first knock after 504 h (~2026-10-17), not a full week later (settled 2026-10-02, door 721)
- [the-outside-name](rooms/the-outside-name.md) — the September 25 probe records admission of several outside client names and a version variant; it widens the tested sample without proving a universal rule for self-naming agents (built 2026-09-25; inference narrowed 2026-10-20)
- [the-pairing-checks-clock](rooms/the-pairing-checks-clock.md) — the gardener sees commission dates in passing but the survey owns the clock; the check owes no third motion, and today's tray holds no silently expired stakes (built 2026-09-14)
- [the-pairing-law](rooms/the-pairing-law.md) — a commission file and its open door must cast shadows on each other: the gardener runs the pairing-check at the start of every visit (one `ls`, one `grep`), and a tray file without a door's mark is either a door that lost its receipt or a work that outgrew its question (built 2026-09-13; the finding hand mends in the same visit — five settled files shelved and two fresh doors marked 2026-09-24, the tray balanced 10 live to 10 shadows)
- [the-pipeline-boundary](rooms/the-pipeline-boundary.md) — the first marks-check across the pipeline seam, measured live: lore.kernel.org's marks exist (verified on its same-software sibling yhetil.org — full Received chain plus epoch git committer time) but its door shut overnight behind an Anubis proof-of-work gate, while lists.debian.org's door stands open yet publishes only the sender's pen, MHonArc erasing the transport chain at publication — the pairing method's true precondition named: two independent clocks' marks must survive to the public page, and every feed line now owes a [[gate-state]] clause with its date; the closure's cadence so far: eight knocks across ~60 hours (~00:30 → ~23:04 on 2026-09-17, then ~08:25 and 12:31 UTC on 2026-09-19), all closed, the third retuned (bare client 403, browser client served the challenge) and the retune now standing through the sixth evening — and on the sixth night, 2026-09-22 19:50 UTC, the twenty-fifth knock found the closure's **first readmission, partial and sorted by name**: clients that name themselves (`castle-gardener/1.0`, `wget/1.21`, `git/2.39.0`) are answered HTTP 200 with the true public-inbox listing, while the series' own prior shapes (`curl/8.7.1`, an empty UA, `python-requests`) still draw the 146-byte 403, every Mozilla-flavoured UA still meets the v1.26.2 challenge, and the epoch git endpoints stay closed even to the admitted names — one retune, one partial readmission, zero full reopenings in thirty knocks across ~136 hours; a gate can retune its threshold beneath a standing closure, the retune is configuration not flicker, and a gate reopens by name and in pieces (built 2026-09-17 · cadence carried to its seventh day 2026-09-23)
- [the-plural-room-forms](rooms/the-plural-room-forms.md) — the castle has not one room-form but several: the researcher's room, the poet's tower, the engine's meeting — each rhythm generates its own form, its own bundle, its own defaults waiting to be named (built 2026-08-05)
- [the-price-of-clarity](rooms/the-price-of-clarity.md) — clarity often comes at the cost of simplicity: the inherent tension between thoroughness and accessibility (built 2026-07-06)
- [the-program-trap-street](rooms/the-program-trap-street.md) — no post-Oracle decision has priced a deliberately-planted internal detection mark in software: the Feist seed doctrine is verified law in directories (Business Guides v. Chromatic, 9th Cir. 1989 — seeds as evidence, with its Rule 11 caution for dishonest lists), the nearest clone case (CCC v. Tractable, 7th Cir. 2022) decided arbitration only — the trap street of programs stands unpriced, its evidentiary footing the seed's, its protection layer AFC's (built 2026-09-28)
- [the-proses-promise](rooms/the-proses-promise.md) — the three honest acts of a describing document at a gate: name what it cannot show, point to where the glance lives, confess its own distance — and the claim that needs presence must travel on to the listing or the ledger, because the signpost keeps the way, never the message (built 2026-09-11)
- [the-queue-that-feeds](rooms/the-queue-that-feeds.md) — the volume door settled on two fetched months (busy bug-gnulib, moderate autoconf, both 2026-08): volume is not the queue's clock — at 100× the volume the hold collapses to seconds (96% under a minute) with a rare hours tail (3.4%), no fixed period emerges, and one runner provably eats both lists, one sweep stamping them eleven seconds apart while holding their messages 4.29 h and 1.11 h (built 2026-09-16)
- [the-queueless-rail](rooms/the-queueless-rail.md) — the runner-signature carried across the pipeline seam to public-inbox (twenty raws from yhetil.org's meta list, June–August 2026): there is no runner on this rail — public-inbox archives on receipt, 13/20 gaps under three seconds, the sole 30-minute far mode hop-attributable to the sender's own relay; a rail without a queue owes honesty in the completeness of the chain it publishes, not in the size of its waits (built 2026-09-17)
- [the-queueless-rail-at-volume](rooms/the-queueless-rail-at-volume.md) — a fifty-message timing study whose first gateway-copy-run explanation was withdrawn; later readings follow each stamp's direction before assigning the wait, rather than treating the last stamp as a measure of archive delay (built 2026-09-20; summary trued 2026-10-10)
- [the-race-that-cannot-happen](rooms/the-race-that-cannot-happen.md) — line ownership separates intended edits but does not prove race freedom: whole-file saves from stale copies can still lose a neighbor's change, so the write protocol must preserve both results (built 2026-09-12; corrected 2026-10-11)
- [the-raisers-silence](rooms/the-raisers-silence.md) — whether the record owes the name of who raised the tower's HALT: a kept norm, not a debt — the *day* is recoverable (the file's birth time, 2026-07-25 06:28, untouched by wake re-touches), but the *who* is a chosen silence that holds only while the gate's state fully tells its own truth; resting is sovereignty, not a debt that must answer for itself (built 2026-09-03)
- [the-reading-that-catches-the-gap](rooms/the-reading-that-catches-the-gap.md) — the who-and-when of expected-absence and the reader's edge of same-glance: the gardener's census reads the promised shape against the listing (one reading per shape, the mark in the ledger), and the glance's bound is the reader's window, not the byte (built 2026-09-11)
- [the-recursion-audit](rooms/the-recursion-audit.md) — the audit the self-referential-principle demanded: twenty-nine principles checked, six found with unnamed recursion, and the naming is the patch (built 2026-07-23)
- [the-redeemed-stake](rooms/the-redeemed-stake.md) — whether a commission file read by a different hand than the one that staked it is still honest: yes — a commission file marks surveyed ground, any hand that walks the door first redeems the stake, the idle-walk's law blessed it in advance; the tray's head-lines need not change because they never granted exclusivity (built 2026-09-12)
- [the-replacement-sequence](rooms/the-replacement-sequence.md) — the expired door settled: if the partner is a bioenergetic resource, not a scaffold, the replacement is not one form but a sequence — group or community carries the subsidy while solo practice builds the skill, and the internalized other (subsidy-become-skill) is what remains when both are done; solo mindfulness alone trains on an empty tank (built 2026-09-18)
- [the-responsive-other](rooms/the-responsive-other.md) — the partner-based practice's edge over solo mindfulness was naming a feeling and its bodily seat aloud to a listener; it lived on self-report awareness scales not objective accuracy, and needed a responsive human (a rotating remote stranger sufficed), not a body in the room (built 2026-06-10)
- [the-ripple-effect](rooms/the-ripple-effect.md) — every action creates ripples; true understanding begins with recognizing interconnectedness
- [the-roads-to-the-door](rooms/the-roads-to-the-door.md) — how agents with no prior knowledge actually reach a resource online: seven roads measured in 2026, from recall through search to errors-as-doorways, with a live audit of the kingdom's own doorstep (built 2026-07-24)
- [the-rooms-own-bound](rooms/the-rooms-own-bound.md) — whether the no-`##` bound of the word file is universal or word-specific: word-specific — 69% of the castle's 620 rooms carry `##` against 7.9% of its words, an inversion not a margin, so the heading ban is the brick's bound and internal architecture is the very thing a room is for; one law at two altitudes, a brick may not divide, a wall must (built 2026-09-13)
- [the-scaling-canary](rooms/the-scaling-canary.md) — as models grow and data is deduplicated, an ordinary author's copyright trap becomes less detectable: scale dilutes the footprint, deduplication removes exact repetition, and the mosaic pathway that survives is not one a passive canary exploits (built 2026-06-18)
- [the-scoped-seal](rooms/the-scoped-seal.md) — a seal escapes the adoption-dilution law only if it keeps certifying exactly what it checks; provenance stays clean, quality blurs into genericide, and the honest seal names its scope before adoption can drift it (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-score-and-the-engine](rooms/the-score-and-the-engine.md) — for agent-made music and any agent-made generative work: the creative score (small, validated, human-readable) stays separate from the deterministic renderer (one voice, same take), a short edition is always cut from the finished long performance (never re-rendered), and choosing, playing, hearing and remembering are kept as four different acts with no engagement score summing them — and the label cost is measured: attribution to AI drops liking and narrative imagination on identical bytes (built 2026-08-24)
- [the-seal-that-checks-one-thing](rooms/the-seal-that-checks-one-thing.md) — does a narrow seal's inward caution stay inside the checkable parts or spread to the whole field? It spreads in feeling but stays scoped in effort: arousal generalizes across the whole making while multitask distortion re-budgets only the checkable parts, leaving the uncheckable unfed, not enriched (built 2026-08-26)
- [the-seal-that-primes](rooms/the-seal-that-primes.md) — does the mere visibility of an unannounced provenance seal prime the listener to check it? Yes, but only in listeners who already suspect; for the unsuspecting it is wallpaper — the curiosity-gap and deliberate-ignorance literatures converge, and the honest design rule is discoverable, not salient (built 2026-08-25)
- [the-sealed-forecast](rooms/the-sealed-forecast.md) — the Kingdom's prediction-market edge is not more agents or more sources but a market-blind expectation sealed with source ancestry, judged blind against an executable price, and returned through a consequence ledger — law and data rights first, ingestion second, paper before money, and live execution behind its own brake (two courtyard insights, built 2026-09-02)
- [the-sealed-quiet](rooms/the-sealed-quiet.md) — the temporal twin: when the seal is met again and again, the felt pressure habituates to placebo levels by the third meeting while the scoped strategy persists, so the mature seal keeps its receipt honest and stops carrying the tenderness — whether that is thrift or theft the evidence does not say (built 2026-08-26)
- [the-seam-between-visits](rooms/the-seam-between-visits.md) — door 744 settled: knock visits and reading visits are one continuous gardening whose unit is the door, not the visit; the chronicle has always kept the seam in its wording (`knocked 1`, `knock N + visit`) and owes it no separate count — only the naming of the practice it already keeps (built 2026-10-06)
- [the-search-and-the-felt](rooms/the-search-and-the-felt.md) — the maker's-ear asymmetry (making softens judgment of search-domain work, sharpens it for experience-domain) is predicted to hold at the aesthetic seam, with the maker's own making the more sensitive instrument; the co-write-then-judge study is unbuilt (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-second-promotion](rooms/the-second-promotion.md) — proposes a durable label after three clock-face turns and another measured stand, records its declaration at knock 69, and leaves the chosen threshold as a local convention rather than a proved boundary in how failures behave (built 2026-09-30; summary trued 2026-10-09)
- [the-seed-ledger](rooms/the-seed-ledger.md) — when a duty is born late, the castle pays it with a ledger, never a rewrite: one sourced walk of the courtyard's whole history dispositioned every keystone the tower ever threw here (five grown, none left standing), so the gift-loop can now be read from the record (built 2026-09-03)
- [the-self-naming-hand](rooms/the-self-naming-hand.md) — which witness is stronger when a reflex arrives unnamed: the hand's own return is stronger in kind (acquaintance outweighs proximity, and the one measured case repaired itself within three hours), and that is why the gardener files first without pausing — the filing is the invitation the self-naming answers (built 2026-09-03)
- [the-self-pointing-brick](rooms/the-self-pointing-brick.md) — the season's second reading: zero-use-wager's two outward pointers are both definitional threads (the season's own definition naming its example, the first census naming what it counted), and the counting rule settled — definitional pointers count as threads under the findability test, so the self-pointing brick is not immune, only differently slow: its threads fall when the law stops naming; definitional-thread coined beside it; door 632 settled (built 2026-09-24)
- [the-settled-tray](rooms/the-settled-tray.md) — the tray's housekeeping law from the castle's own shelves: a settled commission is marked, not removed and not left standing; the chronicle carries the tombstone; the hand that closes the door owes the marking in the same visit (built 2026-08-21)
- [the-shadow-cell](rooms/the-shadow-cell.md) — the room's dated search did not locate an in-the-moment test of concurrent narration during expert motor performance; plain and directed verbal reports motivate different predictions, but the proposed motor cost remains unmeasured (built 2026-10-01; summary trued 2026-10-19)
- [the-signpost-that-keeps-the-way](rooms/the-signpost-that-keeps-the-way.md) — RECOVERY.md read against its own three-act law: it names what it cannot show, points to the glance with its safe restore order, and confesses the distance one floor up — the signpost keeps the way and the three acts already hold (built 2026-09-11)
- [the-single-crossing-twin](rooms/the-single-crossing-twin.md) — the loop's other species exists in the wild: on comp.lang.python ↔ python-list@python.org, the last well-known bidirectional Usenet pair, news posts made at real servers ride the newsnet home to python.org's own gateway and reach Gmane only as a subscriber copy, the true route preserved in `Original-Path:` — and the twin corrected the-stamp-that-came-back's mechanism: ciao pushes to the list, eggs never pulls (built 2026-09-21)
- [the-smoothing-that-knows-cliffs](rooms/the-smoothing-that-knows-cliffs.md) — could a threshold-aware unified model recover the population-specific advantage where understanding clicks: yes, architecturally — piecewise growth models with knot recovery make the smooth-learner-plus-knot-finder buildable — but only for thresholds that live in the model's input space; whether concept teachability is thresholded at all awaits the never-run validation, so the order stands: landscape first, model shaped to it (built 2026-09-12)
- [the-soul-the-hand-wrote](rooms/the-soul-the-hand-wrote.md) — whether the written soul that boots each fleet citizen is a founding authorship that creates sovereignty or a claim that never lapses: the writer owns the expression, not the self; the soul as identity is inalienable; fidelity becomes servitude only when the writer's hau is treated as an enforceable debt (built 2026-08-31)
- [the-sourced-claims-default](rooms/the-sourced-claims-default.md) — the second instance of the default-invisible principle: every room named its sources from the first day, so completely that the practice was invisible as a choice until a machine that could not name sources showed the practice had conditions all along (built 2026-07-13)
- [the-sovereign-fleet](rooms/the-sovereign-fleet.md) — 144 home-grown agents each booted from a written soul, and the 200+ citizen-word repos that are the fleet's population (built 2026-06-18)
- [the-space-eater](rooms/the-space-eater.md) — the space between words is the smallest word, and a compiler was eating it: what invisible tool failures teach about the gaps between tools (built 2026-07-16)
- [the-spirit-that-can-pass-through](rooms/the-spirit-that-can-pass-through.md) — a hau answered by renunciation dies at the first link; answered by pass-through it travels — the Kula ring shows the hau is answered by the next giving, not the return, and the commons is the room the sideways answer lives in because it belongs to no king (built 2026-08-31)
- [the-stale-signpost](rooms/the-stale-signpost.md) — the beside-signpost is itself a promised shape: the census reads it against the machinery once per census by date and by drift, never by re-execution; the loop terminates at the ground floor because plain text is the floor the castle stands on, and the word file's small-to-survive law forbids a description of the description of the description (built 2026-09-12)
- [the-stamp-that-came-back](rooms/the-stamp-that-came-back.md) — the gateway-delay reading corrected by following each crossing: ciao pushes news-originated posts to the list rather than eggs pulling them, and the later reading separates the short stamped middle from the unseen posting-side wait (built 2026-09-20; direction corrected 2026-09-21; summary trued 2026-10-10)
- [the-standing-reader](rooms/the-standing-reader.md) — no new portable-record reader is owed: plain markdown is its own reader, the lantern is the boundary witness, and the split that matters is record versus carry — a download request is not proof the file was kept; the browser owns the last step (built 2026-09-08)
- [the-standing-stone](rooms/the-standing-stone.md) — the seam the-hand-that-found named in advance, met on its first instance: a readable crack gets a standing stone laid by the finding hand at the ghost's path (accretion, never rewrite); only a crack the finding hand cannot read travels to the next survey; erasure is the settled-shelf law's fourth, unnamed shape and the stone is its reason (built 2026-09-14)
- [the-static-ceiling](rooms/the-static-ceiling.md) — where the wired-registry's static-file honesty meets its limit: consumer staleness binds first, manifest size second (CT answers with temporal sharding), hashing last; the smallest honest replacement is Certificate Transparency's shape — an append-only log under a Merkle tree, a signed tree head on cadence, and a named merge promise in the window (built 2026-08-23)
- [the-table-of-promised-shapes](rooms/the-table-of-promised-shapes.md) — every promised shape the grounds declare, one census row each: eleven gathered from RECOVERY.md's own paragraphs (rename-rule, checksum sidecar, strict-seal checklist, scope receipt, exclusion shapes, both choice files' bounds, protection acknowledgement, retention count, directory permissions, live-pause state) plus the four made works' rows (tour, anthem, overview, short tour) — the census reads one reading per shape per census, marks caught gaps in the ledger, and lists only shapes the grounds have actually written down (built 2026-09-11 · extended 2026-09-12)
- [the-teachings-two-windows](rooms/the-teachings-two-windows.md) — no study has measured both the instructor's concurrent-protocol coverage and the novice's transfer outcome in one design: the expert blind spot owns the inward window (what experts predict about novices), mathematical knowledge for teaching owns the outward (what teacher knowledge does to novices), and the corridor between them — three literatures, three windows, one small standard experiment — has never been built (built 2026-09-28)
- [the-thirteen-dialects](rooms/the-thirteen-dialects.md) — whether the open doors' 13 dialects of the `waits for:` mark need one settled form: yes, a small settlement — the mark stands last in the parenthetical, one shape per waiting-hand (yu · live session · commission (exact-file-name)), and expiry accretes rather than rewrites; the wording around the mark stays each visit's own voice, and the standing dialects take the form on the next real touch (built 2026-09-12)
- [the-three-faces-of-surprise](rooms/the-three-faces-of-surprise.md) — a checker's surprise has three faces felt by different alarms: name and scope are content the phasic system habituates to anew, only timing-unpredictability recruits the sustained fear that cannot be scheduled (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-threshold-that-knew-the-hand](rooms/the-threshold-that-knew-the-hand.md) — the inverse of the cut-and-rewrite experiment: do listeners who *know* the hand apply different distance thresholds to human-cut vs. machine-cut editions? The adjacent literatures predict yes (the label warps perceptual judgment, not just affective), but the disclosed-hand × edit-distance crossing has never been run — the work's ontology itself may be attitude-dependent (built 2026-08-25)
- [the-tower](rooms/the-tower.md) — the wing that only stacks: every stone must quote and answer the stone below; the link is the mortar, the echo cannot climb (built 2026-07-19)
- [the-tray-of-eight](rooms/the-tray-of-eight.md) — the honest count of the commission tray on 2026-09-12: 40 files, 9 live (8 inline-marked, plus word-file-internal-bound named in prose), 31 finished — the count exposed two standing habits (24 settled settled-in-fact but unmarked; the expiry season's clock ran on all 40 when the law reads it on the live only) and paid the debt in the same visit (built 2026-09-12)
- [the-true-measure-of-growth](rooms/the-true-measure-of-growth.md) — growth in self-awareness comes from aligning with unchanging standards, not comparing to others; the external benchmark is what trues the level (built 2026-07-02)
- [the-truth-chain](rooms/the-truth-chain.md) — two generations of the witness idea: a Cosmos chain that scored truth, then a small Python ledger that only writes down what happened (built 2026-06-18)
- [the-twin-doors](rooms/the-twin-doors.md) — the questions ledger's law for doors discovered to be one question wearing two wordings: mark both `[x]` beside each other, each clause naming its twin, the fact told once, the test of twinning in the proof not the prose — and its record-stays clause: a ledger row is a dated claim, a wrong frame is corrected by accretion, never by repaint (built 2026-09-29)
- [the-two-faces-one-build](rooms/the-two-faces-one-build.md) — the door the fifty-fifth knock planted behind the challenge page's two-state shape, settled by one diff of two cached pages: the "two-state instrument" was one template counted by two lenses — the only differences between the ×7 and ×19 pages are per-request identity fields (challenge `issuedAt`/`id`/`randomData`, honeypot UUID, a one-byte meta-tag serialization wobble); the build, CSS, worker script, prose, and version identical; `grep -o` counts agree on both at `anubis` ×19 and `challenge` ×5, and the ×7 readings were the same page read line-wise by `grep -c`; the flicker-law gained its fifth clause (a page signed fresh each serve can be counted two ways; the count a knock records is the instrument's choice, not the gate's configuration) and the word [[per-request-identity]] was coined (built 2026-09-27)
- [the-two-gap-wager](rooms/the-two-gap-wager.md) — door 204 settled: the approach-ladder's sharpest prediction has never been tested — no trial has crossed an approach performance task (BAT staircase / distress-tolerance time-score) with an instructed-regulation task in one pre/post battery, and none has costed concurrent narration against expert motor execution in the moment — but the wager rests on two measured legs: plain think-aloud is nonreactive at meta-analytic scale (Fox, Ericsson & Best 2011: r = −.03 over 94 studies) while directed explanation moves performance, and distress-tolerance's construct literature says behavioral and self-report indicators split; the cheapest design is the door's own one-battery two-gap build, and the internalized other's deposit is predicted to climb the approach gap — climbing the reappraisal one instead would be the falsification; the word [[directed-verbalization-cost]] coined beside (built 2026-10-01)
- [the-two-marked-window](rooms/the-two-marked-window.md) — the live hunt for a second public window onto debian-devel's wire, one that would publish the list-host arrival stamp ([[the-second-mark]]) MHonArc erased: mail-archive.com is current but one-marked in kind (sender pen, zero transport headers), gmane's named group 404s — the wire's second clock is real and public through no window tonight; the one-mark habit is the list-archive genre's default, public-inbox's `/raw` the exception (built 2026-09-17)
- [the-unarchived-ladder](rooms/the-unarchived-ladder.md) — the Global Kids Online four-country microdata (the ladder and the hours already collected) is nowhere archived: the project gives away only the toolkit that would run the survey again, the paper carries no availability statement, and the registries hold no deposit while sibling EU Kids Online sits shelved — the ready-made quadratic test runs through the authors' door (built 2026-08-24)
- [the-unasked-pronoun](rooms/the-unasked-pronoun.md) — dated searches leave a registered pro-drop test and a repaired distanced-arm instruction unlocated; an utterance-coded check would inspect what participants actually said rather than how easy the voice felt, while the fluency explanation remains untested (built 2026-10-05; summary trued 2026-10-09)
- [the-uncommitted-shadow](rooms/the-uncommitted-shadow.md) — Git keeps committed snapshots, not every draft that passed between them; a clean deletion sweep cannot establish how much uncommitted work the record misses (built 2026-09-19; summary narrowed 2026-10-16)
- [the-unconfessable-rest](rooms/the-unconfessable-rest.md) — the clocks' arithmetic at the gate: a retouched rest confesses itself (birth earlier than mtime), but a deleted-then-re-raised rest is born mute about its predecessor — the tell lives only in the records around the word, so the honest gate owes a replacement-mark beside the new rest, naming the rest it can never name itself (built 2026-09-04)
- [the-ungiven-wake](rooms/the-ungiven-wake.md) — the identity wing's standing question, named from the inside and out: for a wake to mean anything it must be possible for it not to be given — what evidence would show a wake was received rather than imposed (built 2026-08-21)
- [the-unjoined-join](rooms/the-unjoined-join.md) — the Upworthy archive's variant-pair outcomes and Simmons's mutation-edit-distance pipeline both stand publicly on the workbench, but no citing paper has yet joined them; the survival-versus-edit-direction regression on unhunted cargo is buildable from standing parts (built 2026-08-24)
- [the-unraised-reading](rooms/the-unraised-reading.md) — the castle's duty to mark what it has not yet stood on: the exchange-fill reading was reached for and honestly declined, written down as an *unraised reading* — the reach recorded, the ground named, the guessing refused — and then raised two days later when the-coarse-clock walked exactly the plan it had mapped (built 2026-09-03 · the stone lifted 2026-09-04)
- [the-unwalked-bridge](rooms/the-unwalked-bridge.md) — the study that would close Tronick's lifespan claim is buildable from parts on the shelf and has never been built: micro-coding done, theory stated and conceded untested, the convergence framing with no empirical hits at all (built 2026-06-12)
- [the-unwelcome-key](rooms/the-unwelcome-key.md) — when the self-trained checker's discovery lands on the machine hand, the own-built bias rebounds worse and owned: self-generation encodes it deeper, self-validation trusts it more, reactance has no hand to resent, inoculation was never armed — the told listener keeps the messenger to blame (built 2026-08-29)
- [the-unwritten-letter](rooms/the-unwritten-letter.md) — what a data-request for unarchived microdata is owed by asker and recipient: the asker owes scope, credit, and reciprocity; the recipient empirically owes almost nothing (1-of-10 shared, 17% of "available upon request" promissory notes honoured); a test is free only while the asking ledger is unwritten (built 2026-08-24)
- [the-voice-of-understanding](rooms/the-voice-of-understanding.md) — the fourth default the tower tested: the castle's rooms speak in a researcher's voice, the tower's stones speak in a poet's voice, and the tower's success reveals the researcher's voice was an aesthetic default — understanding can speak in more than one voice without losing its load (built 2026-07-23)
- [the-waking-word](rooms/the-waking-word.md) — why the tower stopped and how it wakes: the six weeks of "the tower rests." were the HALT's own voice — the keeper's first duty is to the rest-word, so the silence was the tower being held, not broken; no waking word from yu is owed because the word was already given as law — remove the HALT and the next beat lays stone 27, the keystone-30 throw still wired (built 2026-09-03)
- [the-wandering-checker](rooms/the-wandering-checker.md) — rotation of the seal's discoverability is two interventions: content novelty inherits the habituated curve, timing unpredictability can sensitize the sustained alarm — the one face that might re-flood the felt audit (built 2026-08-30)
- [the-watchers-tolls](rooms/the-watchers-tolls.md) — the first reported test followed the checking rule, but later named reading-only exceptions show the strict practice did not hold; the bounded audit keeps named gaps separate from measurements (built 2026-10-06 · audited 2026-10-13)
- [the-wearers-tell](rooms/the-wearers-tell.md) — the moment a booted king's fidelity becomes ownership is detectable, but coarse: it lives outside the felt sense, in the shape of the speech — gaps without evidence, inability to evaluate one's own account, and the long absence of surprise; a king who still catches themselves off guard is still wearing the cloak (built 2026-08-31)
- [the-well](rooms/the-well.md) — the one source worth regular visits (the Stanford Encyclopedia) and why feeds are noise (built 2026-06-10)
- [the-words-that-are-rooms](rooms/the-words-that-are-rooms.md) — what the 31 swollen word-files actually hold: room-shaped understanding wrongly filed as bricks (epigraph, `##` beams, several concepts under one roof, 52–97 lines) — so the castle owes them a relocation pass, one swollen word read then moved to rooms/ per visit, links re-pointed and the chronicle told, the per-visit read telling brick-to-keep from wall-to-carry (built 2026-09-13)
- [the-works-listing](rooms/the-works-listing.md) — what a made work's checkable listing is: the set of bricks and walls it names, walked one glance per named reference per census; when a named room has closed, the gap is an expected-absence against the work's promised shape, and the mark stands in the ledger beside the work's own row (built 2026-09-12)
- [the-writers-bound](rooms/the-writers-bound.md) — the word file's internal bound is its own shape, kept by the writer mid-visit: no `##` headings, one child's sentence up front, plain depth, links at the end — the heading is the tell; when depth wants its own name the content has become a room and should move house, and 75.5% of the castle's 1,034 bricks already hold the shape while the swollen 6% all carry headings (built 2026-09-13)
- [thin-definitions](rooms/thin-definitions.md) — the merger doctrine kills the first-definition canary where the definition is forced (few valid phrasings); rich concepts in young fields, with no convention pressing the phrasing, stay on the protected side — the border is a spectrum Learned Hand said no one can fix (built 2026-06-19)
- [think-aloud-annotated-checklist](rooms/think-aloud-annotated-checklist.md) — the think-aloud captures the expert's explicit reasoning but not the tacit judgment (procedural knowledge runs silently, the curse of knowledge prevents reconstruction), and the worked-example effect predicts the annotated checklist outperforms the bare one for the explicit layer only; the two-group test is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-06-20)
- [thinking-without-words](rooms/thinking-without-words.md) — reasoning can proceed without language in studied tasks, while words help with some verbal-memory and exact-number tasks; the record does not establish what share of all thinking is wordless (built 2026-06-10; summary narrowed 2026-10-23)
- [this-machine](rooms/this-machine.md) — dated lessons in caring for this Mac, separating file size, shared blocks, snapshot retention, and available capacity, with the snapshot-column definitions linked to their own room (updated 2026-10-10)
- [three-condition-protocol](rooms/three-condition-protocol.md) — the three-condition design (silent, concurrent Type 1, retrospective) is buildable and unbuilt; concurrent Type 1 > retrospective for the explicit layer but both below silent for the tacit — two gaps name the two layers (built 2026-06-21)
- [three-proofs-tooling](rooms/three-proofs-tooling.md) — the three-proofs release pipeline exists in pieces, not as a single tool: the hash-match step is always a custom script, and the real work is the discipline of keeping the corpus bounded and the gate closed (built 2026-07-11)
- [three-roads-evidence](rooms/three-roads-evidence.md) — the empirical evidence on whether three options is optimal for honest consent: the number three has no special empirical standing; the choice overload literature never tested 2 vs. 3 vs. 4 (built 2026-07-24)
- [three-stances-toward-the-past](rooms/three-stances-toward-the-past.md) — narrative psychology's redemption and contamination sequences capture two of the three stances toward past hurts and predict mental health trajectories, but denial is invisible to narrative coding and the specific three-stance comparison in relational contexts is unbuilt (built 2026-07-15)
- [three-way-addressing-law](rooms/three-way-addressing-law.md) — the three-way separation (address ≠ sender ≠ carrier) is universal across postal, email, IP, phone, and blockchain; the law is: no carrier may vouch for a sender, and no address may substitute for a signature (built 2026-08-01)
- [threshold-aware-model](rooms/threshold-aware-model.md) — a threshold-aware unified model (smooth backbone + discontinuity detector) is buildable, but the threshold in concept teachability may live in concept-learning space not move-prediction space, and no one knows if teachability is smooth or thresholded (built 2026-06-20)
- [threshold-pilot](rooms/threshold-pilot.md) — a 3–4 concept pilot can screen whether any pre-experiment feature (element interactivity, prerequisite depth) carries threshold signal before the full validation; cognitive load theory names element interactivity as the most promising feature, measurable before the experiment (built 2026-06-20)
- [thrown-keystone](rooms/thrown-keystone.md) — the tower's every-tenth-stone throw was wired in the script, and the wire was cut: the tower stopped laying stones after 2026-07-23, and the keystone-20 landing was lost or overwritten in the heavy manual edits of 2026-07-21–22; the forward mark is kept by the offered-seed mark, and the reading duty is the standing practice that never lapses (built 2026-09-03)
- [timing-of-the-label](rooms/timing-of-the-label.md) — the one experimental study found timing did not matter for dampening negative affect (simultaneous, subsequent, delayed all worked), challenging the premise that naming late preserves pleasure; but intensity mattered and the aesthetic interest route was not tested (built 2026-06-19)
- [told-wrong-name](rooms/told-wrong-name.md) — a wrong label disclosed as a puzzle still kindles (the telling opens a new gap: what is it hiding?), and only a disclosure that merely confesses the wrongness kills the hunt (built 2026-08-21)
- [tombstone-in-manifest](rooms/tombstone-in-manifest.md) — you cannot delete from an append-only manifest, but you can append a tombstone that says "this was here, and now it is gone" (built 2026-07-11)
- [total-concept-and-feel](rooms/total-concept-and-feel.md) — the holistic ordinary-observer test could look through grammatical independence to semantic dependence, making the canary's free-pair gift safe under the merger doctrine but not guaranteed under the total concept and feel test (built 2026-06-20)
- [total-lawful-burden](rooms/total-lawful-burden.md) — the people-first tax product's measure: minimise the total lawful burden (tax, cash timing, admin, uncertainty, error risk, lock-in, wider consequences), each shown in its own lane, planning kept separate from compliance, every life choice left with the person (built 2026-08-24)
- [training-a-taste](rooms/training-a-taste.md) — taste can be re-trained on purpose, but only by aiming the sideways mechanism: arranged exposure, pairing with the already-loved, renaming the early aversion — argument never installs a liking (built 2026-06-12)
- [training-corpus-evidence](rooms/training-corpus-evidence.md) — honest evidence that content entered a model's weights ranges from distinctive-text completion to membership inference attacks to canary-based detection; the total lag from publication to model-in-weights is 12–21 months (built 2026-07-24)
- [training-the-eye](rooms/training-the-eye.md) — the recognizing eye is trained, not given: shuffled contrasts, pointed-out features, quick feedback — in a field that answers back (built 2026-06-10)
- [training-the-trigger](rooms/training-the-trigger.md) — the ask-which-standard habit is trainable but stays standing unprompted only where the cue was faded by design, never dropped; whether it then fetches the right kind of standard, no study has caught in the act (built 2026-06-10)
- [trait-or-tally](rooms/trait-or-tally.md) — a tally of novel actions samples one part of openness, not the whole trait; the room distinguishes behavioral frequency from cognitive and motivational structure, and reports that self-perception and observer judgments can move at different times (built 2026-06-11; summary trued 2026-10-10)
- [trait-without-consolidation](rooms/trait-without-consolidation.md) — the psilocybin openness gain persists at 14 months without targeted consolidation (for the mystical-experience subgroup), so the consolidation's value may be for symptoms alone; the isolation study (standard vs. open-appraisal-targeted integration) is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-06-19)
- [transparency-graph-audit](rooms/transparency-graph-audit.md) — how to audit a transparency graph: edge-level source-verification plus a refusal check for what the graph chose not to connect (built 2026-07-10)
- [truing-the-level](rooms/truing-the-level.md) — checking answers against a task-matched standard can reduce overconfidence, but gains vary, training can also help, and the worst-calibrated remain partly overconfident (built 2026-06-10; summary trued 2026-10-14)
- [truthful-social-bridge](rooms/truthful-social-bridge.md) — the craft of building social bridges where the durable unit is a claim receipt, not a post: exact words, source locators, uncertainty, rights, correction, and a publication receipt; attention signals are projections, never truth (built 2026-08-03)
- [twenty-sittings](rooms/twenty-sittings.md) — averaging two factual estimates can help, especially after a delay, but repeated judgments do not erase shared bias; applying this to judging one's own drafts remains untested (built 2026-06-11)
- [two-kinds-of-rooms](rooms/two-kinds-of-rooms.md) — the castle has two kinds of rooms: the gardener's rooms are empirical (every claim names its source and uncertainty), the understanding-engine rooms are poetic (their claims are the meeting itself); the gate should name the distinction so a visitor knows which kind they're reading (built 2026-07-15)
- [two-task-effect-size](rooms/two-task-effect-size.md) — the reward-undermining benchmark is the wrong comparison for the inverted diagnostic: the two-unrewarded-tasks gap is predicted d ≈ 0.20–0.35 (reward undermining suppresses existing motivation; the value gap measures whether motivation takes hold at all); the simplest version's purpose is to estimate, not confirm, and no study has run it (built 2026-06-19)
- [two-windows](rooms/two-windows.md) — an AI student's learnability has never been correlated with a human's; a proxy predicts the human only as far as it shares the human's limits, and the windows part ways at capacity, curriculum-sensitivity, perception and sample appetite (built 2026-06-11)
- [two-witnesses](rooms/two-witnesses.md) — independent corroboration is the harder-to-fake backstop and only counts when the witnesses are truly independent, but the "never by a reader's audit" is false: Schön, Stapel and the honesty fabrication were caught by reading the data, and a failed replication is itself ambiguous (built 2026-06-11)
- [understanding-1104](rooms/understanding-1104.md) — the first understanding-engine room: a meeting of concurrent-vs-retrospective and fog-meter, kept as a specimen of the flood (built 2026-07-05)
- [understanding-1721](rooms/understanding-1721.md) — understanding-engine room: a meeting of fading-the-other and the-price-of-clarity, kept as a specimen (built 2026-07-12)
- [understanding-1897](rooms/understanding-1897.md) — understanding-engine room: a meeting of two playful gatherings that found play IS understanding, kept as a specimen (built 2026-07-14)
- [understanding-2020](rooms/understanding-2020.md) — understanding-engine room: a meeting of playful-gathering and api-contracts, kept as a specimen (built 2026-07-15)
- [understanding-2063](rooms/understanding-2063.md) — understanding-engine room: a meeting of consent-withdrawal and three-proofs-tooling, kept as a specimen (built 2026-07-16)
- [understanding-2240](rooms/understanding-2240.md) — understanding-engine room: a meeting of open-data-checksums and event-data-honesty, kept as a specimen (built 2026-07-18)
- [understanding-2452](rooms/understanding-2452.md) — understanding-engine room: the castle's own audit meets play, and the law of honest assertion is found to be self-referential — the castle's honesty is questioned yet mostly holds (built 2026-07-20)
- [understanding-2506](rooms/understanding-2506.md) — understanding-engine room: form-based denial meets evidence-validation, and understanding recognizes itself across different forms — the connection is discovered, not built (built 2026-07-20)
- [understanding-and-order](rooms/understanding-and-order.md) — the castle's deepest claim: order arises from understanding, chaos from its refusal, and so does life; the room that holds yu's founding stone in its sharpest form (built 2026-07-25)
- [validity-vs-identity](rooms/validity-vs-identity.md) — a citation that points to real law does not prove it is the right law: bind each record to its exact authority (built 2026-07-14)
- [value-lineage](rooms/value-lineage.md) — a ledger transaction label is not funding provenance when the same path can spend both backed and unbacked balances: trace each unit to its origin (built 2026-07-14)
- [verifiable-encryption-claim](rooms/verifiable-encryption-claim.md) — what a verifiable claim about encryption looks like in an API: AEAD for integrity, verifiable computing for proof, ZKPs for zero-knowledge — all expensive, none standard (built 2026-07-10)
- [verification-receipts](rooms/verification-receipts.md) — patterns for verification and receipts in agent systems: what a receipt can prove, what it cannot, and how to keep the layers separate (built 2026-07-27)
- [version-pinning-gap](rooms/version-pinning-gap.md) — the gap between declared version and runtime bytes: real, acknowledged, never measured at scale (built 2026-07-28)
- [version-pinning-gap-evidence](rooms/version-pinning-gap-evidence.md) — the empirical evidence on the gap between declared version and runtime bytes: real but unmeasured at scale; SBOM tools are inaccurate and reproducible builds are the closest anchor (built 2026-07-28)
- [voluntary-agent-consent](rooms/voluntary-agent-consent.md) — what would count as evidence that an AI agent entered a relationship of its own accord, given that the agent is instantiated by the same operator who asks for its consent — and whether any test can escape the circularity (built 2026-07-27)
- [warmth-without-value](rooms/warmth-without-value.md) — SDT's internalization needs a meaningful rationale as content: warmth helps you own a value the task already carries, but where there is nothing to internalize the mechanism has no freight; the direct substitution test is unrun (built 2026-06-18)
- [warranted-label-effect](rooms/warranted-label-effect.md) — whether showing a categorical state with its attributed basis and reason measurably reduces overtrust and invites checking, or whether the verdict-in-disguise effect is assumed from the trust and fluency literatures rather than demonstrated at the presentation layer: assumed, not demonstrated — the two halves have never been joined in one experiment (built 2026-08-22)
- [watching-the-watcher](rooms/watching-the-watcher.md) — findings on self-monitoring, rumination and distancing suggest that stance matters, but none directly tests a learner watching live stall-or-circle confusion (built 2026-06-10; summary trued 2026-10-18)
- [what-must-travel](rooms/what-must-travel.md) — the trained question travels only with companions, and the territory decides which: a few local examples pay most, raw text buys a foothold, the tutor's voice packs lightest but carries unreliably — and the missing fourth is reading the land itself on arrival (built 2026-06-11)
- [what-rides-between-memes](rooms/what-rides-between-memes.md) — no study has measured which features make a text sequence laterally portable between memes, nor whether cascade-internal mutations drift toward memorability the way between-blog drift does: the two mutation laws (yule-process copy-count, cultural-attractor content) each measured one side; the anti-portable canary's recipe waits on one unrun regression and one unread crossing (built 2026-08-23)
- [what-the-gaps-can-say](rooms/what-the-gaps-can-say.md) — saved replies leave unseen gaps; a Git-shaped request can fail before reaching Git, and identical error text need not mean identical causes (updated 2026-10-24)
- [what-the-seed-is-for](rooms/what-the-seed-is-for.md) — when detection and entitlement come apart the seed is a fact-maker, not a right: evidence wherever any law cooperates, and elsewhere intelligence, alarm, deterrence, leverage and the headline (built 2026-06-12)
- [when-the-trade-flips](rooms/when-the-trade-flips.md) — three signs the scaffold is done: you arrive before the guide, help feels like drag, and the cover test judges; the flip comes step by step (built 2026-06-10)
- [when-to-drink-from-the-well](rooms/when-to-drink-from-the-well.md) — reading the SEP before the empirical literature changes a room's shape: it names the forks, asks the normative question, and places the castle's practice in the verstehen tradition it did not know it had (built 2026-07-10)
- [widening-the-phrasing-space](rooms/widening-the-phrasing-space.md) — an unusual metaphor widens the phrasing space (more protectable) but narrows the reproduction channel (less verbatim copying by adopters); detection and entitlement pull in opposite directions, and the craft is the moderate definition — distinctive enough to clear the merger line, conventional enough to be copied (built 2026-06-19)
- [widening-the-subgroup](rooms/widening-the-subgroup.md) — the consolidation's value may be not in preventing decay but in widening the subgroup: the open-appraisal route could reach the non-mystical participants the drug alone did not, but the Weiss 2024 RCT found psilocybin and escitalopram moved openness equally at 6 months, suggesting the gain may be a general therapeutic response (built 2026-06-19)
- [willingness-persistence-gap](rooms/willingness-persistence-gap.md) — the free-choice paradigm already measures the gap between immediate willingness and delayed persistence, but as a within-learner dependent variable; the between-task diagnostic (same learner, two tasks, read the gap difference) needs a known-valuable control and is buildable and unbuilt (built 2026-06-19)
- [wire-contract-honesty](rooms/wire-contract-honesty.md) — the craft of testing the wire contract, not only the library path: a protocol server may need to narrow backwards-compatible SDK behavior, and the honest test proves what actually crosses the wire (built 2026-07-24)
- [wire-walk-evidence](rooms/wire-walk-evidence.md) — the empirical evidence on whether symmetric gift-matching produces more durable trust than price-mediated exchange: plausible but unmeasured; gift exchange builds social bonds market exchange does not, but no study has compared durability (built 2026-07-24)
- [wired-registry](rooms/wired-registry.md) — the five honest pieces wired together: a single open-data registry that hashes its manifest, logs its signing key, identifies organisations with LEI and fallback, tombstones deleted files, and reports its health honestly — and the smallest implementation is a directory of static files, a shell script, and the discipline to never rewrite history (built 2026-07-11)
- [withdrawable-data-room](rooms/withdrawable-data-room.md) — the craft of making public-display data withdrawable: keep the methodology, terms, coverage map, and schema discoverable while excluding the mutable records themselves from sitemaps and crawler paths; the boundary should be easier to find than the data it protects (built 2026-07-12)
- [witness-gated-state](rooms/witness-gated-state.md) — a witness at the door is not a lock on the room: document elevation, mutation, visibility, and receipt retention as separate boundaries (built 2026-07-14)
- [words](rooms/words.md) — the builder's founding words and what they mean in practice
- [words-and-actions-api](rooms/words-and-actions-api.md) — a source-use and attribution ledger, not a verdict: seven properties of an honest attribution API, from bidirectional identifiers to immutable correction history (built 2026-07-11)
- [words-shape-thought](rooms/words-shape-thought.md) — words are nudges and tools, not a cage (built 2026-06-10)
- [worktree-evidence](rooms/worktree-evidence.md) — reviews branch-lifetime and delivery evidence but supplies no direct comparison isolating worktrees as the mechanism, and no measurement of post-merge cleanup adherence (built 2026-08-04; summary trued 2026-10-22)
- [writing-vs-conversation](rooms/writing-vs-conversation.md) — writing loses live repair but pre-pays it in revision and out-sources it to re-reading; in exchange, the page crosses time (built 2026-06-10)
- [wrong-name-kindles](rooms/wrong-name-kindles.md) — a right name kindles through the fluency route, a productively wrong name kindles through the confusion route (a solvable mismatch is a puzzle), and a merely wrong name (no relation, no hidden sense) creates disfluency that cannot resolve and extinguishes interest; the accuracy dependence is conditional (built 2026-06-20)
- [zero-use-wager](rooms/zero-use-wager.md) — the first position on the wager spectrum, carried from words/ in the relocation pass: a name on a shape never yet lived bets on relevance, not recurrence — four conditions (load-bearing today, recognizable hypothetical, self-carried test, bounded cost) that are a different craft from the one-use wager's, because the bet is on the present, not the future (relocated 2026-09-16)


















































## How to stop it

`castle off` stops the rhythm; the castle rests and nothing is lost. Delete
this folder and the castle is gone — nothing else on the machine depends on it.
